    // whatever text/URL is posted to it, nothing account-specific is read or
    // stored), so no requireAccount() gate — same reasoning as any other
    // pure-utility endpoint. Shells out to backend/qr_gen.py (OpenCV's
    // QRCodeEncoder) rather than a hand-rolled or npm QR encoder — see that
    // file's own header comment for why. Returns a data: URL so the front
    // end can drop it straight into an <img src>.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 2 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'qr'){
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const data = typeof body.data === 'string' ? body.data : '';
      if (!data || !data.trim()){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'data is required' });
      }
      if (data.length > 2000){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'data is too long to encode into a single QR code (max 2000 characters)' });
      }
      try {
        const b64 = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          const proc = spawn('python3', [path.join(__dirname, 'qr_gen.py')]);
          let out = '', errOut = '';
          proc.stdout.on('data', c => { out += c; });
          proc.stderr.on('data', c => { errOut += c; });
          proc.on('error', reject);
          proc.on('close', code => {
            if (code !== 0) return reject(new Error(errOut || `qr_gen.py exited ${code}`));
            resolve(out);
          });
          proc.stdin.write(data);
          proc.stdin.end();
        });
        return sendJson(res, 200, { dataUrl: `data:image/png;base64,${b64}` });
      } catch (e){
        return sendJson(res, 500, { error: 'QR generation failed: ' + e.message });
      }
    }

    // POST /api/utm-links/validate — Round 132av, QR Code & UTM step's real
    // link testing. Runs server-side (not client fetch()) specifically to
    // avoid CORS — a browser can't read the response status of a
    // cross-origin fetch to an arbitrary marketing/landing-page domain, so
    // client-side "testing" could only ever fake a result. Body: { urls:
    // string[] } (deduped, max 50 per call so one bad batch can't hang the
    // request indefinitely). For each URL: HEAD first (cheaper), falling
    // back to GET if the host doesn't support HEAD (some do a blanket 405).
    // 8s timeout per URL via AbortController; anything that throws
    // (DNS failure, connection refused, timeout) reports ok:false with the
    // error message rather than crashing the batch.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'utm-links' && parts[2] === 'validate'){
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const urls = Array.isArray(body.urls) ? [...new Set(body.urls.filter(u => typeof u === 'string' && u.trim()))].slice(0, 50) : [];
      const results = await Promise.all(urls.map(checkLinkResolves));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { results });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/landing-page-audit — Round 132av, per direct
    // instruction: "The URL is nice for analytics but not CX... add the
    // content read." Real server-side fetch of the campaign's actual
    // landing page (not just URL-string parsing), split into the same two
    // named halves the instruction asked for: Relevance (content + imagery
    // vs. this campaign's own messaging — AI-scored, honestly null when no
    // ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is configured) and Performance (does the CTA link
    // actually resolve, and does its destination path fit the conversion
    // KPI type — both real, mechanical checks, no AI needed, so these work
    // today regardless of whether a key is configured). Per direct
    // clarification, this round only rebuilds the Landing Page & Tracking
    // component — the separate Creative & Messaging component (Press
    // Release/Social/Email project validation) is untouched here.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'landing-page-audit'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const url = campaign.campaignUrl;
      if (!url) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'This campaign has no Campaign URL set yet.' });
      let pageHtml, fetchError = null;
      try {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000);
        const resp = await fetch(url, { redirect: 'follow', signal: controller.signal });
        clearTimeout(timer);
        if (!resp.ok){ fetchError = `Page returned HTTP ${resp.status}.`; }
        else { pageHtml = await resp.text(); }
      } catch (e){
        fetchError = 'Could not fetch the page: ' + (e.message || 'request failed');
      }
      if (fetchError){
        return sendJson(res, 200, { fetchOk: false, fetchError, runAt: new Date().toISOString() });
      }
      const page = extractLandingPageContent(pageHtml, url);
      const [ctaLinkCheck, contentRelevance, imageRelevance] = await Promise.all([
        page.cta && page.cta.href ? checkLinkResolves(page.cta.href) : Promise.resolve(null),
        scoreContentRelevance(page, campaign),
        scoreImageRelevance(page.heroImageUrl, campaign)
      ]);
      const ctaPath = ctaPathCoherence(page.cta && page.cta.href, campaign.conversionType);
      const result = {
        fetchOk: true,
        runAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        page: { title: page.title, metaDescription: page.metaDescription, headline: page.headline, heroImageUrl: page.heroImageUrl, cta: page.cta },
        relevance: { content: contentRelevance, imagery: imageRelevance },
        performance: {
          ctaFound: !!(page.cta && page.cta.href),
          ctaLinkResolves: ctaLinkCheck ? { ok: ctaLinkCheck.ok, status: ctaLinkCheck.status, error: ctaLinkCheck.error || null } : null,
          ctaPathCoherence: ctaPath
        }
      };
      db.prepare('UPDATE campaigns SET landingPageAuditJson = ?, landingPageAuditAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(JSON.stringify(result), result.runAt, campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, result);
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/messaging-relevance-score — round 132[aov-poc]
    // (2026-08-17). Real AI judgment of whether this campaign's drafted
    // Long Form Copy will actually produce its Loop Stage's business
    // outcome — built in direct response to the AOV Copy Variation proof of
    // concept, which showed the deterministic Messaging QA score cannot
    // measure that on its own. See scoreMessagingBusinessOutcomeRelevance()
    // for the full rationale. Manually triggered (like the landing-page
    // audit above), not run on every keystroke — a real model call per
    // click, not per render. Accepts the live draft text in the body so it
    // can score a draft before it's been saved/approved.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'messaging-relevance-score'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const draftCopy = typeof body.draftCopy === 'string' ? body.draftCopy : (campaign.longformCopy || '');
      const keyMessage = typeof body.keyMessage === 'string' ? body.keyMessage : (campaign.keyMessage || '');
      const result = await scoreMessagingBusinessOutcomeRelevance(campaign, draftCopy, keyMessage);
      const runAt = new Date().toISOString();
      const payload = { ...result, runAt };
      db.prepare('UPDATE campaigns SET messagingRelevanceJson = ?, messagingRelevanceScoredAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(JSON.stringify(payload), runAt, campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, payload);
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/messaging-ai-draft — round 132[aov-poc]
    // (2026-08-17). Real AI-generated Long Form Copy, built in direct
    // response to "why were the copy outputs so much better when I ran the
    // exercise outside of this platform" — see generateMessagingCopyViaAI()
    // for the full rationale. This does NOT overwrite anything server-side;
    // it returns a draft for the frontend to offer alongside the
    // deterministic template draft, same "show real output, let a human
    // choose/approve" pattern as every generative feature in this build.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'messaging-ai-draft'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(campaign.accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const result = await generateMessagingCopyViaAI(campaign, account, {
        keyMessage: typeof body.keyMessage === 'string' ? body.keyMessage : (campaign.keyMessage || ''),
        // 2026-08-22 — falls back to this campaign's saved Role/Style
        // selection (see the roleStyle ensureColumn() comment) when the
        // caller doesn't explicitly override it, so the new campaign-level
        // selector actually reaches this prompt instead of only being
        // stored and never read.
        style: (typeof body.style === 'string' && body.style) ? body.style : (campaign.roleStyle || ''),
        focus: typeof body.focus === 'string' ? body.focus : ''
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { ...result, runAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/style-assets — round 20, Brand Foundations
    // (Style Guide: Colors & Fonts). Merge-update, same convention as the
    // other account-level Brand Foundations endpoints. `approved` is set
    // whenever the request includes it as true (the frontend sends this
    // once at least one meaningful field has been filled in and Save is
    // clicked — see portal.html's saveStyleAssets()).
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'style-assets'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const pick = (key) => body[key] !== undefined ? body[key] : existing[key];
      const merged = {
        logoUrl: pick('logoUrl'),
        logoDataUrl: pick('logoDataUrl'),
        logoFileName: pick('logoFileName'),
        logoUsageNotes: pick('logoUsageNotes'),
        primaryColorName: pick('primaryColorName'),
        primaryColorHex: pick('primaryColorHex'),
        secondaryPaletteText: pick('secondaryPaletteText'),
        colorHierarchyNotes: pick('colorHierarchyNotes'),
        headingFontName: pick('headingFontName'),
        headingFontNotes: pick('headingFontNotes'),
        bodyFontName: pick('bodyFontName'),
        bodyFontNotes: pick('bodyFontNotes'),
        typographyHierarchyNotes: pick('typographyHierarchyNotes'),
        styleNotes: pick('styleNotes'),
        styleSourceFileName: pick('styleSourceFileName')
      };
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const approved = body.approved ? 1 : (existing.styleAssetsApproved || 0);
      const approvedAt = body.approved ? now : existing.styleAssetsApprovedAt;
      db.prepare(
        `UPDATE accounts SET logoUrl = ?, logoDataUrl = ?, logoFileName = ?, logoUsageNotes = ?, primaryColorName = ?, primaryColorHex = ?, secondaryPaletteText = ?,
         colorHierarchyNotes = ?, headingFontName = ?, headingFontNotes = ?, bodyFontName = ?, bodyFontNotes = ?,
         typographyHierarchyNotes = ?, styleNotes = ?,
         styleSourceFileName = ?, styleAssetsApproved = ?, styleAssetsApprovedAt = ? WHERE accountId = ?`
      ).run(
        merged.logoUrl, merged.logoDataUrl, merged.logoFileName, merged.logoUsageNotes, merged.primaryColorName, merged.primaryColorHex, merged.secondaryPaletteText,
        merged.colorHierarchyNotes, merged.headingFontName, merged.headingFontNotes, merged.bodyFontName, merged.bodyFontNotes,
        merged.typographyHierarchyNotes, merged.styleNotes,
        merged.styleSourceFileName, approved, approvedAt, accountId
      );
      return sendJson(res, 200, { approved: !!approved, approvedAt });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/positioning — round 22, Brand Foundations
    // (Competitive Positioning), the third and final gate component. Same
    // merge-update convention as /voice, /profile, /style-assets.
    // `competitors` arrives as an array (<=5 entries) and is stored as a
    // JSON string — a small, fixed-size list that's always read/written as
    // a whole, not worth a join table for.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'positioning'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const industryTrendsText = body.industryTrendsText !== undefined ? body.industryTrendsText : existing.industryTrendsText;
      const competitorsJson = Array.isArray(body.competitors)
        ? JSON.stringify(body.competitors.slice(0, 5))
        : existing.competitorsJson;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const approved = body.approved ? 1 : (existing.competitivePositioningApproved || 0);
      const approvedAt = body.approved ? now : existing.competitivePositioningApprovedAt;
      db.prepare(
        `UPDATE accounts SET industryTrendsText = ?, competitorsJson = ?, competitivePositioningApproved = ?,
         competitivePositioningApprovedAt = ? WHERE accountId = ?`
      ).run(industryTrendsText, competitorsJson, approved, approvedAt, accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { approved: !!approved, approvedAt });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/keywords — round 32, Search Everywhere keyword
    // list. The whole array is replaced on every save (same convention as
    // /positioning's competitors array) — the front end always sends the
    // full current list (after any removals), not a diff.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'keywords'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!Array.isArray(body.keywords)) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'keywords[] is required' });
      db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET searchKeywordsJson = ? WHERE accountId = ?')
        .run(JSON.stringify(body.keywords), accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/brand-keywords — round 32, follow-on. Brand &
    // Product Keywords (Brand Voice page) — { brand: [], product: [],
    // negative: [] }, whole-object replaced on every save, same convention
    // as /keywords above.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'brand-keywords'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const brandKeywords = {
        brand: Array.isArray(body.brand) ? body.brand : [],
        product: Array.isArray(body.product) ? body.product : [],
        negative: Array.isArray(body.negative) ? body.negative : []
      };
      db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET brandKeywordsJson = ? WHERE accountId = ?')
        .run(JSON.stringify(brandKeywords), accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }

    // Round 102 — account-configurable taxonomy (Product Group, Creative
    // Market, Audience, Buy Type, Media Type dropdown lists), per the
    // scoping doc's Section 3: this is AOV's real, already-in-production
    // vocabulary becoming editable per-account data instead of a hardcoded
    // travel-specific enum.

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/taxonomies — every taxonomy row on file
    // for this account (populates the Channel Planning Detail card's
    // dropdowns; empty array for a brand-new account that hasn't defined any
    // yet, per the doc's "new accounts start empty" note).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'taxonomies'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM account_taxonomies WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY taxonomyKey ASC').all(accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { taxonomies: rows.map(r => ({ ...r, valuesJson: JSON.parse(r.valuesJson) })) });
    }

    // PUT /api/accounts/:accountId/taxonomies/:taxonomyKey — upsert one
    // taxonomy's label + ordered value list. Body: { label, values: [...] }.
    // Whole-list replace (same "upsert whole set" convention as Media Plan
    // allocations/keywords elsewhere in this file) rather than incremental
    // add/remove — order matters (it's dropdown order), so the caller always
    // sends the full ordered list it wants.
    if (req.method === 'PUT' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'taxonomies'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const taxonomyKey = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const values = Array.isArray(body.values) ? body.values.filter(v => typeof v === 'string' && v.trim()) : [];
      const label = typeof body.label === 'string' && body.label.trim() ? body.label.trim() : taxonomyKey;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM account_taxonomies WHERE accountId = ? AND taxonomyKey = ?').get(accountId, taxonomyKey);
      if (existing){
        db.prepare('UPDATE account_taxonomies SET label = ?, valuesJson = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
          .run(label, JSON.stringify(values), now, existing.id);
        return sendJson(res, 200, { id: existing.id, updatedAt: now });
      }
      const id = generateId('TAX');
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO account_taxonomies (id, accountId, taxonomyKey, label, valuesJson, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)')
        .run(id, accountId, taxonomyKey, label, JSON.stringify(values), now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/taxonomy-templates — quick-start Product
    // Group / Creative Focus Group starting points, per direct instruction
    // 2026-08-22: "This brings up a time saving option. Let's provide
    // clients with an option to begin with a quick start option when
    // creating the groups... We should start with travel but then expand to
    // include best guesses for each NAICS code. Brands can override
    // everything and start with a fresh upload or edit what we provide."
    // Deliberately just a hardcoded registry, not a DB table — same
    // no-fabrication discipline as everywhere else in this file: only real,
    // directly-specified content ships (the Cruise Line and Travel Agency
    // templates below, verbatim from that instruction), nothing invented
    // for other NAICS codes yet. TAXONOMY_TEMPLATES is where those get added
    // as they're built — see its own comment. Returns the FULL registry
    // (not account-scoped/filtered) since there's no reliable per-account
    // NAICS mapping yet to match against — the client picks whichever fits,
    // same "brands can override everything" instruction. Still gated behind
    // requireAccount since it's only ever reached from inside the
    // authenticated portal, same posture as every other account-scoped GET
    // in this file, even though the payload itself isn't account-specific.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'taxonomy-templates'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      return sendJson(res, 200, { templates: TAXONOMY_TEMPLATES });
    }

    // Round 102 — Marketing Calendar (doc Section 5). Reads straight off
    // channel_planning_details: every entry already carries Hit Date (the
    // date a piece actually reaches its audience — the doc calls this out as
    // the meaningful marketing-impact date, same treatment MMM already gives
    // Hit/start date) plus Channel/Partner/Product Group/Creative Market, so
    // no new fields were needed beyond what the Section 1/3 tables above add.
    // getMarketingCalendarEvents() is shared by the JSON route and both
    // export routes below so all three return the exact same event set for
    // the same query params.
    function getMarketingCalendarEvents(accountId, q){
      let sql = `SELECT cpd.id, cpd.campaignId, c.name AS campaignName, c.objective AS campaignObjective,
          cpd.channel, cpd.partner, cpd.hitDate, cpd.dropDate, cpd.endDate,
          cpd.productYear, cpd.productGroup, cpd.creativeMarket, cpd.audience, cpd.budget, cpd.status
        FROM channel_planning_details cpd
        JOIN campaigns c ON c.id = cpd.campaignId
        WHERE c.accountId = ?`;
      const params = [accountId];
      if (q.start){ sql += ' AND cpd.hitDate >= ?'; params.push(q.start); }
      if (q.end){ sql += ' AND cpd.hitDate <= ?'; params.push(q.end); }
      if (q.channel){ sql += ' AND cpd.channel = ?'; params.push(q.channel); }
      if (q.productGroup){ sql += ' AND cpd.productGroup = ?'; params.push(q.productGroup); }
      if (q.creativeMarket){ sql += ' AND cpd.creativeMarket = ?'; params.push(q.creativeMarket); }
      if (q.campaignId){ sql += ' AND cpd.campaignId = ?'; params.push(q.campaignId); }
      sql += ' ORDER BY cpd.hitDate ASC';
      const rows = db.prepare(sql).all(...params);
      // Same "name || objective" fallback portal.html already uses everywhere
      // else a campaign needs a display label (see e.g. its goal step and
      // campaign-list rows) — matched here so the calendar's campaignTitle
      // never disagrees with what the campaign is called anywhere else in
      // the product.
      return rows.map(r => ({
        id: r.id,
        campaignId: r.campaignId,
        campaignTitle: r.campaignName || r.campaignObjective || '(untitled campaign)',
        channel: r.channel,
        partner: r.partner,
        hitDate: r.hitDate,
        dropDate: r.dropDate,
        endDate: r.endDate,
        productYear: r.productYear,
        productGroup: r.productGroup,
        creativeMarket: r.creativeMarket,
        audience: r.audience,
        budget: r.budget === null ? null : Number(r.budget),
        status: r.status
      }));
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/marketing-calendar/export.xlsx — must be
    // matched BEFORE the plain marketing-calendar GET below (parts.length
    // 5 vs 4, but checked first regardless so route order stays obviously
    // correct if either length check is ever loosened).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-calendar' && parts[4] === 'export.xlsx'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const events = getMarketingCalendarEvents(accountId, {
        start: url.searchParams.get('start') || '', end: url.searchParams.get('end') || '',
        channel: url.searchParams.get('channel') || '', productGroup: url.searchParams.get('productGroup') || '',
        creativeMarket: url.searchParams.get('creativeMarket') || '', campaignId: url.searchParams.get('campaignId') || ''
      });
      const headers = ['Campaign', 'Channel', 'Partner', 'Drop Date', 'Hit Date', 'End Date', 'Product Year', 'Product Group', 'Creative Market', 'Audience', 'Budget', 'Status'];
      const rows = events.map(e => [e.campaignTitle, e.channel, e.partner || '', e.dropDate || '', e.hitDate || '', e.endDate || '', e.productYear || '', e.productGroup || '', e.creativeMarket || '', e.audience || '', e.budget === null ? '' : e.budget, e.status || '']);
      // Genuine .xlsx binary, not CSV-with-an-.xlsx-name — generated by
      // xlsx_gen.py (openpyxl), same "shell out to what's actually available
      // in this sandbox" pattern qr_gen.py already established, since this
      // sandbox's npm registry access is blocked (403 on exceljs/xlsx/
      // node-xlsx, same as every other package this file's header notes).
      try {
        const b64 = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          const proc = spawn('python3', [path.join(__dirname, 'xlsx_gen.py')]);
          let out = '', errOut = '';
          proc.stdout.on('data', c => { out += c; });
          proc.stderr.on('data', c => { errOut += c; });
          proc.on('error', reject);
          proc.on('close', code => {
            if (code !== 0) return reject(new Error(errOut || `xlsx_gen.py exited ${code}`));
            resolve(out);
          });
          proc.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ sheetName: 'Marketing Calendar', headers, rows }));
          proc.stdin.end();
        });
        const buf = Buffer.from(b64, 'base64');
        res.writeHead(200, {
          'Content-Type': 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
          'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename="marketing-calendar.xlsx"',
          'Content-Length': buf.length,
          'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
        });
        return res.end(buf);
      } catch (e){
        return sendJson(res, 500, { error: 'xlsx export failed: ' + e.message });
      }
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/marketing-calendar/export.csv — same
    // event set as export.xlsx, RFC4180-quoted UTF-8 CSV. This is what the
    // front end's "Open in Google Sheets" affordance downloads and hands to
    // Sheets' own File > Import (Google Sheets has no public unauthenticated
    // create-a-sheet API this backend could call on the client's behalf
    // without OAuth credentials this app doesn't have — so the CSV download
    // IS the integration point, not a workaround).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-calendar' && parts[4] === 'export.csv'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const events = getMarketingCalendarEvents(accountId, {
        start: url.searchParams.get('start') || '', end: url.searchParams.get('end') || '',
        channel: url.searchParams.get('channel') || '', productGroup: url.searchParams.get('productGroup') || '',
        creativeMarket: url.searchParams.get('creativeMarket') || '', campaignId: url.searchParams.get('campaignId') || ''
      });
      const headers = ['Campaign', 'Channel', 'Partner', 'Drop Date', 'Hit Date', 'End Date', 'Product Year', 'Product Group', 'Creative Market', 'Audience', 'Budget', 'Status'];
      const csvCell = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const lines = [headers.map(csvCell).join(',')];
      events.forEach(e => {
        lines.push([e.campaignTitle, e.channel, e.partner || '', e.dropDate || '', e.hitDate || '', e.endDate || '', e.productYear || '', e.productGroup || '', e.creativeMarket || '', e.audience || '', e.budget === null ? '' : e.budget, e.status || ''].map(csvCell).join(','));
      });
      const csv = '﻿' + lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n'; // BOM so Excel/Sheets reliably detect UTF-8
      res.writeHead(200, {
        'Content-Type': 'text/csv; charset=utf-8',
        'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename="marketing-calendar.csv"',
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
      });
      return res.end(csv);
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/marketing-calendar — the calendar's data
    // source. Query params: start, end (ISO dates, both inclusive, matched
    // against hitDate), optional channel/productGroup/creativeMarket/
    // campaignId filters — same filter vocabulary as the rest of Marketing
    // Ops per the doc's Section 5, not calendar-specific filters.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-calendar'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const events = getMarketingCalendarEvents(accountId, {
        start: url.searchParams.get('start') || '', end: url.searchParams.get('end') || '',
        channel: url.searchParams.get('channel') || '', productGroup: url.searchParams.get('productGroup') || '',
        creativeMarket: url.searchParams.get('creativeMarket') || '', campaignId: url.searchParams.get('campaignId') || ''
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { events });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/campaigns — list this account's campaigns + their projects
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'campaigns'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { campaigns: getCampaignsForAccount(accountId) });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/campaign-smart-defaults — 2026-08-22, roadmap
    // item 3: "confidence-gated intake — the fun, not intimidating engine."
    // Per direct instruction: the moment Verilume has enough signal to be
    // confident about an account, it should hand the client a shortcut
    // instead of re-asking a form it can already answer.
    //
    // Extended same day, later round — per the vision doc's own next-step
    // note on this item: move past "borrow the last campaign's value" toward
    // genuine adaptive question-skipping. Two real confidence tiers now,
    // computed independently per field (a field can be 'skip'-confident while
    // another on the same account is only 'suggest'-confident, or has
    // nothing at all — never forced to move together):
    //   'skip'    — the last 3+ campaigns that actually set this field all
    //               agree on the exact same value. That's real, repeated
    //               signal, not a one-off — confident enough to hand the
    //               field back already filled in, not just offered as a
    //               click-away suggestion. Still fully editable; never
    //               hidden or locked, per the same "a wrong silent guess is
    //               worse than no guess" discipline as the original design.
    //   'suggest' — only the most recent campaign has a value (today's
    //               original behavior, unchanged) — one prior data point
    //               isn't enough to auto-fill, but it's still worth a
    //               one-click offer rather than silence.
    //   'none'    — nothing on file for this field. Honest, not fabricated.
    // Looks at the account's last 8 campaigns (recency-ordered) per field,
    // using however many of those actually have that field set — a field
    // set on every recent campaign reaches 'skip' faster than one that's
    // only occasionally filled in.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'campaign-smart-defaults'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      // 2026-08-22 — extended past the original two/three fields (Key
      // Message, Audience Targets, Segment) to Lifecycle-Loop Stage, Primary
      // KPI, and Channels — the next natural fields for genuine adaptive
      // question-skipping per this endpoint's own flagged next step.
      // Deliberately Stage, not Objective: Objective is no longer an
      // independently-set field at all (portal.html's cmpCurrentObjective()
      // derives it purely from selectedStage via STAGE_TO_OBJECTIVE — there
      // is no standalone Objective input left to pre-fill), so Stage is the
      // real upstream field worth learning from an account's history.
      // 2026-08-22, later round — roleStyle/keyMessageMode/messageType added
      // to the SELECT. These 3 only became worth learning from an account's
      // history once all 3 actually persist across campaigns (roleStyle/
      // keyMessageMode already did; messageType/mandatoryPhrase just closed
      // that same gap this round — see the ensureColumn() comment above).
      // mandatoryPhrase is deliberately NOT included here: it's real,
      // campaign-specific required text (a promo/legal phrase), not a tone
      // or mode setting a NEW campaign should inherit by default the way
      // Role/Style or Key Message mode reasonably can.
      const recent = db.prepare(
        `SELECT id, name, keyMessage, audienceTargets, segment, stage, primaryKpi, channels, roleStyle, keyMessageMode, messageType, createdAt FROM campaigns
         WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC LIMIT 8`
      ).all(accountId);
      const SKIP_THRESHOLD = 3;
      // Segment is stored as a comma-separated string (see selectedSegments
      // in portal.html), and two campaigns that picked the exact same
      // segments can still have them in a different order — comparing the
      // raw string would treat "Past Customers,Hand-Raisers" and
      // "Hand-Raisers,Past Customers" as a disagreement they aren't.
      // Canonicalize (trim, dedupe, sort, rejoin) before comparing OR
      // returning, so both the streak math and the value handed back to the
      // client are order-independent.
      function canonicalizeSegments(raw){
        const parts = [...new Set(String(raw).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean))].sort();
        return parts.join(',');
      }
      function confidenceForField(fieldName, normalize){
        const norm = normalize || (v => v.trim());
        const withValue = recent
          .filter(c => c[fieldName] && String(c[fieldName]).trim())
          .map(c => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name || c.id, value: norm(String(c[fieldName])) }))
          .filter(c => c.value); // a segment string that canonicalizes to empty (all whitespace/commas) is effectively unset
        if (!withValue.length) return { level: 'none' };
        const mostRecent = withValue[0];
        // 'skip' requires the run of most-recent non-empty values to agree —
        // a single outlier campaign in between (a genuinely different brief)
        // should NOT count toward the streak, so this walks from the front
        // and stops at the first disagreement rather than counting matches
        // anywhere in the 8-campaign window.
        let streak = 0;
        for (const c of withValue){
          if (c.value === mostRecent.value) streak++;
          else break;
        }
        if (streak >= SKIP_THRESHOLD){
          return { level: 'skip', value: mostRecent.value, sourceCount: streak };
        }
        return { level: 'suggest', value: mostRecent.value, source: { campaignId: mostRecent.id, name: mostRecent.name } };
      }
      const fields = {
        keyMessage: confidenceForField('keyMessage'),
        audienceTargets: confidenceForField('audienceTargets'),
        // 2026-08-22, later same round — Segment extension. Deliberately
        // scoped out of the first pass of this feature: Segment isn't a
        // free-text field, it's a multi-select with stage-based eligibility
        // rules (cmpSegmentDisabledSet() in portal.html — e.g. Past
        // Customers is invalid at the Awareness stage). The backend side
        // stays simple (same confidence math as the other two fields, just
        // segment-shaped comparison) — the eligibility filtering happens
        // client-side at apply time, where selectedStage actually lives.
        segment: confidenceForField('segment', canonicalizeSegments),
        // 2026-08-22 extension — Stage and Primary KPI are plain scalar
        // fields, same shape as keyMessage (no eligibility rules to apply
        // client-side), so they use the default `norm` and read back exactly
        // like keyMessage/audienceTargets on the frontend. Channels is
        // multi-select and comma-separated like Segment, so it reuses the
        // same canonicalizeSegments() order-independent comparison — the
        // helper's name predates this field but the comparison logic is
        // identical (dedupe/sort/rejoin a comma list).
        stage: confidenceForField('stage'),
        primaryKpi: confidenceForField('primaryKpi'),
        channels: confidenceForField('channels', canonicalizeSegments),
        // 2026-08-22, later round — Role/Style, Key Message mode, and
        // Message Type are the next natural fields per this endpoint's own
        // "next natural fields" pattern (each prior extension picked up
        // whatever had just become real/persisted). All 3 are plain scalar
        // fields, same shape as stage/primaryKpi — no eligibility rules to
        // apply client-side, so the default `norm` is fine.
        roleStyle: confidenceForField('roleStyle'),
        keyMessageMode: confidenceForField('keyMessageMode'),
        messageType: confidenceForField('messageType')
      };
      const confident = Object.values(fields).some(f => f.level !== 'none');
      if (!confident){
        return sendJson(res, 200, { confident: false, reason: 'No prior campaign with a Key Message, Audience Targets, Segment, Lifecycle-Loop Stage, Primary KPI, Channels, Role/Style, Key Message mode, or Message Type on file yet — nothing to suggest from.', fields });
      }
      return sendJson(res, 200, { confident: true, fields });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/campaigns — create a campaign under this account
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'campaigns'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      // Round 132c17 — selecting partnerCode too (not just accountId), since
      // generateCampaignCode() below needs it; this was the one bug found
      // during smoke-testing this round — an accountId-only SELECT here
      // silently produced a null Campaign Code even with a real Partner
      // Code on file.
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId, partnerCode FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      // Round 132d follow-on (2026-08-09) — Objective moved off Campaign
      // Creation and into the new, locked-until-created Lifecycle Stage &
      // Goals stage (it's filled in and saved there, after this POST
      // establishes the real ID). Requiring it here would break every
      // campaign the client now creates, since the client genuinely has
      // nothing to send yet — so this now accepts an empty objective (the
      // `objective` column stays NOT NULL, satisfied by ''), same honest
      // "collected later" pattern the rest of this stage already uses.
      const campaignId = generateId('CMP');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      // Round 132c17 — Campaign Code (see generateCampaignCode()'s comment).
      // productCode is derived from body.productName if the caller didn't
      // send an explicit 3-letter code; account.partnerCode comes from
      // Company Profile, not from this request body.
      const productName = body.productName || '';
      const productCode = threeLetterCode(body.productCode || productName);
      const campaignCode = generateCampaignCode(accountId, account.partnerCode, productCode, body.startDate || null);
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO campaigns (id, accountId, objective, segment, stage, keyMessage, name, budget, plannedImpressions, startDate, endDate, isAdHoc, functions, campaignUrl, conversionType, channels, fundingSource, allocationId, demandSignalRef, primaryKpi, kpiGoal, transactionWindowDays, productGroups, creativeFocusGroups, productCode, productName, campaignCode, roleStyle, keyMessageMode, messageType, mandatoryPhrase, createdAt)
         VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`
      ).run(
        campaignId, accountId, body.objective || '', body.segment || '', body.stage || '', body.keyMessage || '',
        body.name || body.objective || 'Untitled Campaign',
        typeof body.budget === 'number' ? body.budget : null,
        typeof body.plannedImpressions === 'number' ? body.plannedImpressions : null,
        body.startDate || null,
        body.endDate || null,
        body.isAdHoc ? 1 : 0,
        body.functions || '',
        body.campaignUrl || '',
        body.conversionType || '',
        body.channels || '',
        body.fundingSource || 'unplanned',
        body.allocationId || null,
        body.demandSignalRef || null,
        body.primaryKpi || '',
        typeof body.kpiGoal === 'number' ? body.kpiGoal : null,
        typeof body.transactionWindowDays === 'number' ? body.transactionWindowDays : null,
        // Round 132w (2026-08-10) — genuinely persisted now; see the
        // ensureColumn() comment above for why this was silently dropped
        // before, despite the front end already sending it.
        body.productGroups || '',
        body.creativeFocusGroups || '',
        productCode || null,
        productName || null,
        campaignCode,
        // 2026-08-22 — roleStyle/keyMessageMode, see the ensureColumn()
        // comment above.
        body.roleStyle || '',
        body.keyMessageMode || 'exact',
        // 2026-08-22 — messageType/mandatoryPhrase, see the ensureColumn()
        // comment above (this round closes the same "session-only" gap for
        // these two that an earlier round already closed for
        // roleStyle/keyMessageMode).
        body.messageType || '',
        body.mandatoryPhrase || '',
        now
      );
      // Round 55 — insert one row per {allocationId, amount} draw when the
      // campaign spans multiple Media Plan allocations. body.allocationId
      // (single, legacy) stays on the campaign row above for any caller
      // still using the round-53/54 shape; allocationDraws is additive.
      if (Array.isArray(body.allocationDraws)){
        const insertDraw = db.prepare('INSERT INTO campaign_allocation_draws (id, campaignId, allocationId, amount) VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
        body.allocationDraws.forEach(d => {
          if (!d || !d.allocationId) return;
          insertDraw.run(generateId('DRW'), campaignId, d.allocationId, typeof d.amount === 'number' ? d.amount : 0);
        });
      }
      return sendJson(res, 201, { campaignId, campaignCode });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id — round 16: update a campaign's lifecycle
    // status and/or its manual Analysis-step inputs (actualSpend,
    // actualImpressions, actualConversions, analysisNotes). Merge-update —
    // any field left out of the request body keeps its existing stored
    // value, so advancing status alone never wipes out previously-saved
    // analysis data (and vice versa).
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const merged = {
        status: body.status !== undefined ? body.status : existing.status,
        actualSpend: body.actualSpend !== undefined ? body.actualSpend : existing.actualSpend,
        actualImpressions: body.actualImpressions !== undefined ? body.actualImpressions : existing.actualImpressions,
        actualConversions: body.actualConversions !== undefined ? body.actualConversions : existing.actualConversions,
        analysisNotes: body.analysisNotes !== undefined ? body.analysisNotes : existing.analysisNotes,
        // Round 31 — Campaign URL & Conversion Tracking, same merge-update
        // convention as every field above.
        campaignUrl: body.campaignUrl !== undefined ? body.campaignUrl : existing.campaignUrl,
        conversionType: body.conversionType !== undefined ? body.conversionType : existing.conversionType,
        // Round 36 — the 7-stage progress bar's two manually-set fields.
        brandStage: body.brandStage !== undefined ? body.brandStage : existing.brandStage,
        qaApproved: body.qaApproved !== undefined ? (body.qaApproved ? 1 : 0) : existing.qaApproved,
        // Round 53 — Media Plan funding fields, same merge-update convention.
        channels: body.channels !== undefined ? body.channels : existing.channels,
        fundingSource: body.fundingSource !== undefined ? body.fundingSource : existing.fundingSource,
        allocationId: body.allocationId !== undefined ? body.allocationId : existing.allocationId,
        budget: body.budget !== undefined ? body.budget : existing.budget,
        // Round 65 — Brand card fields, same merge-update convention.
        // keyMessage previously had no update path at all (set once at
        // creation only); folded in here alongside the three new fields.
        keyMessage: body.keyMessage !== undefined ? body.keyMessage : existing.keyMessage,
        brandToneNotes: body.brandToneNotes !== undefined ? body.brandToneNotes : existing.brandToneNotes,
        brandGuidelines: body.brandGuidelines !== undefined ? body.brandGuidelines : existing.brandGuidelines,
        creativeBrief: body.creativeBrief !== undefined ? body.creativeBrief : existing.creativeBrief,
        longformCopy: body.longformCopy !== undefined ? body.longformCopy : existing.longformCopy,
        // Round 32, part D — recommended/edited channel & audience mix.
        mediaMixJson: body.mediaMixJson !== undefined ? body.mediaMixJson : existing.mediaMixJson,
        // Round 99 — Performance Marketing's validation + the shared
        // Audience Targets field Omni-Channel Creative reads read-only.
        audienceTargets: body.audienceTargets !== undefined ? body.audienceTargets : existing.audienceTargets,
        pmValidatedAt: body.pmValidatedAt !== undefined ? body.pmValidatedAt : existing.pmValidatedAt,
        // Round 132w (2026-08-10) — same merge-update convention, so a
        // later edit (or the one-time backfill script) can update these
        // without needing a dedicated endpoint.
        productGroups: body.productGroups !== undefined ? body.productGroups : existing.productGroups,
        creativeFocusGroups: body.creativeFocusGroups !== undefined ? body.creativeFocusGroups : existing.creativeFocusGroups,
        // Round 132aa — same merge-update convention as every field above.
        approvedAssetJobIds: body.approvedAssetJobIds !== undefined ? body.approvedAssetJobIds : existing.approvedAssetJobIds,
        pmAssetsApprovedAt: body.pmAssetsApprovedAt !== undefined ? body.pmAssetsApprovedAt : existing.pmAssetsApprovedAt,
        approvedAssetSummary: body.approvedAssetSummary !== undefined ? body.approvedAssetSummary : existing.approvedAssetSummary,
        // Round 132av — Creative handoff grid's Active/Complete checkboxes.
        creativeActive: body.creativeActive !== undefined ? (body.creativeActive ? 1 : 0) : existing.creativeActive,
        creativeComplete: body.creativeComplete !== undefined ? (body.creativeComplete ? 1 : 0) : existing.creativeComplete,
        // Round 132be — "Flag this style for future editorial improvements."
        // messagingStyleDigestJson deliberately never carries the literal
        // keyMessage/longformCopy text — see this migration's ensureColumn
        // comment above and portal.html's cmpMessagingStyleDigest().
        messagingTrainingExample: body.messagingTrainingExample !== undefined ? (body.messagingTrainingExample ? 1 : 0) : existing.messagingTrainingExample,
        messagingTrainingExampleAt: body.messagingTrainingExampleAt !== undefined ? body.messagingTrainingExampleAt : existing.messagingTrainingExampleAt,
        messagingStyleDigestJson: body.messagingStyleDigestJson !== undefined ? body.messagingStyleDigestJson : existing.messagingStyleDigestJson,
        // Round 132bx — Cancel campaign action, same merge-update convention.
        cancelled: body.cancelled !== undefined ? (body.cancelled ? 1 : 0) : existing.cancelled,
        cancelledAt: body.cancelledAt !== undefined ? body.cancelledAt : existing.cancelledAt,
        // Round 132c17 — same merge-update convention. productCode/
        // productName can be edited after creation (e.g. a campaign created
        // before this round, or before the account had a partnerCode on
        // file); campaignCode only (re)generates here when it's still unset,
        // so a real, already-issued Campaign Code is never silently
        // regenerated out from under expense rows/job codes that already
        // reference it.
        productCode: body.productCode !== undefined ? threeLetterCode(body.productCode) : existing.productCode,
        productName: body.productName !== undefined ? body.productName : existing.productName,
        // 2026-08-22 — same merge-update convention as every field above.
        roleStyle: body.roleStyle !== undefined ? body.roleStyle : existing.roleStyle,
        keyMessageMode: body.keyMessageMode !== undefined ? body.keyMessageMode : existing.keyMessageMode,
        // 2026-08-22 — messageType/mandatoryPhrase, closing the same
        // session-only gap this round for these two that roleStyle/
        // keyMessageMode already had closed.
        messageType: body.messageType !== undefined ? body.messageType : existing.messageType,
        mandatoryPhrase: body.mandatoryPhrase !== undefined ? body.mandatoryPhrase : existing.mandatoryPhrase
      };
      if (!existing.campaignCode){
        const account = db.prepare('SELECT partnerCode FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(existing.accountId);
        const startDate = body.startDate !== undefined ? body.startDate : existing.startDate;
        merged.campaignCode = generateCampaignCode(existing.accountId, account && account.partnerCode, merged.productCode, startDate);
      } else {
        merged.campaignCode = existing.campaignCode;
      }
      db.prepare(
        'UPDATE campaigns SET status = ?, actualSpend = ?, actualImpressions = ?, actualConversions = ?, analysisNotes = ?, campaignUrl = ?, conversionType = ?, brandStage = ?, qaApproved = ?, channels = ?, fundingSource = ?, allocationId = ?, budget = ?, keyMessage = ?, brandToneNotes = ?, brandGuidelines = ?, creativeBrief = ?, longformCopy = ?, mediaMixJson = ?, audienceTargets = ?, pmValidatedAt = ?, productGroups = ?, creativeFocusGroups = ?, approvedAssetJobIds = ?, pmAssetsApprovedAt = ?, approvedAssetSummary = ?, creativeActive = ?, creativeComplete = ?, messagingTrainingExample = ?, messagingTrainingExampleAt = ?, messagingStyleDigestJson = ?, cancelled = ?, cancelledAt = ?, productCode = ?, productName = ?, campaignCode = ?, roleStyle = ?, keyMessageMode = ?, messageType = ?, mandatoryPhrase = ? WHERE id = ?'
      ).run(merged.status, merged.actualSpend, merged.actualImpressions, merged.actualConversions, merged.analysisNotes, merged.campaignUrl, merged.conversionType, merged.brandStage, merged.qaApproved, merged.channels, merged.fundingSource, merged.allocationId, merged.budget, merged.keyMessage, merged.brandToneNotes, merged.brandGuidelines, merged.creativeBrief, merged.longformCopy, merged.mediaMixJson, merged.audienceTargets, merged.pmValidatedAt, merged.productGroups, merged.creativeFocusGroups, merged.approvedAssetJobIds, merged.pmAssetsApprovedAt, merged.approvedAssetSummary, merged.creativeActive, merged.creativeComplete, merged.messagingTrainingExample, merged.messagingTrainingExampleAt, merged.messagingStyleDigestJson, merged.cancelled, merged.cancelledAt, merged.productCode, merged.productName, merged.campaignCode, merged.roleStyle, merged.keyMessageMode, merged.messageType, merged.mandatoryPhrase, campaignId);
      // Round 55 — same merge-update convention: only touch draws when the
      // caller actually sent an allocationDraws array, and replace them
      // wholesale (delete + reinsert) rather than trying to diff rows, same
      // upsert-whole-plan pattern the Media Plan endpoint already uses.
      if (Array.isArray(body.allocationDraws)){
        db.prepare('DELETE FROM campaign_allocation_draws WHERE campaignId = ?').run(campaignId);
        const insertDraw = db.prepare('INSERT INTO campaign_allocation_draws (id, campaignId, allocationId, amount) VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
        body.allocationDraws.forEach(d => {
          if (!d || !d.allocationId) return;
          insertDraw.run(generateId('DRW'), campaignId, d.allocationId, typeof d.amount === 'number' ? d.amount : 0);
        });
      }
      return sendJson(res, 200, { updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }

    // Round 102 — Channel Planning Detail routes. COST_SUBFIELDS is the
    // "sensitive vendor economics" set from the scoping doc's Section 2 —
    // Unit Cost/Postage/Paper & Manufacturing/Insertion — that only a CX
    // Experiences Media Ops actor (actorRole === 'cx_ops') may set or change,
    // checked here on the server rather than trusted to the front end hiding
    // the fields. These live inside detailsJson (channel-specific sub-fields,
    // e.g. only Direct Mail rows carry them), so the guard below inspects
    // whatever detailsJson keys the caller actually sent.
    const CHANNEL_PLANNING_COST_SUBFIELDS = ['unitCost', 'postageCost', 'paperMfgCost', 'insertionCost'];
    function channelPlanningCostFieldsPresent(detailsJson){
      if (!detailsJson || typeof detailsJson !== 'object') return [];
      return CHANNEL_PLANNING_COST_SUBFIELDS.filter(k => detailsJson[k] !== undefined && detailsJson[k] !== null && detailsJson[k] !== '');
    }
    function serializeChannelPlanningRow(row){
      return {
        ...row,
        impressions: row.impressions === null ? null : Number(row.impressions),
        budget: row.budget === null ? null : Number(row.budget),
        detailsJson: row.detailsJson ? JSON.parse(row.detailsJson) : {}
      };
    }

    // Round 132x (2026-08-10), per direct instruction — the Manage Account
    // bulk uploader (client-facing, per cxmedia-real-demo-environment-
    // marketing-calendar-scoping.md) needs the exact same insert behavior
    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/channel-planning already enforces (cost-field
    // role guard, draft/submitted/approved gate, entered-by stamping) on
    // every one of potentially hundreds of rows, plus a shared "recompute
    // this campaign's rollup" step neither the single-entry POST nor PATCH/
    // DELETE previously did. Pulled both into standalone functions so the
    // bulk path and the single-entry endpoints call one real implementation
    // instead of two copies that could drift.
    //
    // insertChannelPlanningRow: same contract as the POST body this always
    // accepted (actorRole/actorName/channel/partner/audience/buyType/
    // mediaType/impressions/dropDate/hitDate/endDate/productYear/
    // productGroup/creativeMarket/budget/detailsJson/status/allocationId),
    // now also requiring campaignId on the body itself (the single-entry
    // endpoint still gets it from the URL and injects it before calling
    // this). Returns { entryId } on success; throws { status, error } (an
    // HTTP-status-shaped object, not a real Error) on any validation
    // failure, so a bulk caller can catch per-row without one bad row
    // aborting the whole batch.
    function insertChannelPlanningRow(body){
      const campaignId = body.campaignId;
      if (!campaignId) throw { status: 400, error: 'campaignId is required' };
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) throw { status: 404, error: `campaign not found: ${campaignId}` };
      const actorRole = body.actorRole === 'cx_ops' ? 'cx_ops' : 'client';
      const actorName = typeof body.actorName === 'string' ? body.actorName : '';
      if (!body.channel) throw { status: 400, error: 'channel is required' };
      const detailsJson = (body.detailsJson && typeof body.detailsJson === 'object') ? body.detailsJson : {};
      const presentCostFields = channelPlanningCostFieldsPresent(detailsJson);
      if (actorRole !== 'cx_ops' && presentCostFields.length){
        throw { status: 403, error: `Only CX Experiences Media Ops may set vendor cost fields (${presentCostFields.join(', ')}) — clients can view but not edit vendor rate/cost economics.` };
      }
      let status = body.status === 'submitted' || body.status === 'approved' ? body.status : 'draft';
      if (status === 'approved' && actorRole !== 'cx_ops'){
        throw { status: 403, error: 'Only CX Experiences Media Ops may set status to approved.' };
      }
      const entryId = generateId('CPD');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO channel_planning_details
        (id, campaignId, allocationId, channel, partner, audience, buyType, mediaType, impressions,
         dropDate, hitDate, endDate, productYear, productGroup, creativeMarket, budget, detailsJson,
         status, enteredByRole, enteredByName, lastEditedByRole, lastEditedByName, createdAt, updatedAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`
      ).run(
        entryId, campaignId, body.allocationId || null, body.channel,
        body.partner || null, body.audience || null, body.buyType || null, body.mediaType || null,
        typeof body.impressions === 'number' ? body.impressions : null,
        body.dropDate || null, body.hitDate || null, body.endDate || null,
        body.productYear || null, body.productGroup || null, body.creativeMarket || null,
        typeof body.budget === 'number' ? body.budget : null,
        JSON.stringify(detailsJson), status,
        actorRole, actorName, actorRole, actorName,
        now, now
      );
      return { entryId, campaignId };
    }

    // syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning: the live
    // equivalent of the one-time backfill-product-creative-groups.js
    // script — recomputes a campaign's productGroups/creativeFocusGroups
    // as the distinct, real productGroup/creativeMarket values across all
    // of ITS OWN channel_planning_details rows (same "keep every distinct
    // value, don't pick one winner" rule as the backfill script, and same
    // untouched original casing so it keeps matching account_taxonomies
    // exactly). Called after every channel-planning write (bulk insert,
    // single insert, update, delete) so the campaign-level rollup Campaign
    // Creation/Marketing Calendar/the template-recommendation scorer all
    // read is never stale relative to the real line items behind it —
    // this is the live mechanism the backfill script was a one-time stand-
    // in for before this round.
    function syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning(campaignId){
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT productGroup, creativeMarket FROM channel_planning_details WHERE campaignId = ?').all(campaignId);
      // Only recompute when there's at least one real line item to derive
      // from. If the campaign has none (a fresh campaign that hasn't had
      // any Channel Planning Detail rows added yet, or the last one was
      // just deleted), leave campaign.productGroups/creativeFocusGroups
      // exactly as-is rather than blanking out whatever was set directly
      // on Campaign Creation's own Product Group/Creative Focus Group
      // picker (round 132c) — this function only ever ADDS the real,
      // line-item-level picture once one exists; it never erases a
      // campaign-level value that has no line items to check it against.
      if (!rows.length) return;
      const distinctNonEmpty = (col) => {
        const seen = new Set();
        rows.forEach(r => { const v = (r[col] || '').trim(); if (v) seen.add(v); });
        return [...seen];
      };
      const productGroups = distinctNonEmpty('productGroup').join(', ');
      const creativeFocusGroups = distinctNonEmpty('creativeMarket').join(', ');
      db.prepare('UPDATE campaigns SET productGroups = ?, creativeFocusGroups = ? WHERE id = ?').run(productGroups, creativeFocusGroups, campaignId);
    }

    // GET /api/campaigns/:id/channel-planning — list every Channel Planning
    // Detail entry (one per vendor/insertion/drop) for this campaign.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'channel-planning'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM channel_planning_details WHERE campaignId = ? ORDER BY hitDate ASC, createdAt ASC').all(campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { entries: rows.map(serializeChannelPlanningRow) });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/channel-planning — create one Channel Planning
    // Detail entry. Body: { actorRole ('cx_ops'|'client'), actorName,
    // allocationId, channel, partner, audience, buyType, mediaType,
    // impressions, dropDate, hitDate, endDate, productYear, productGroup,
    // creativeMarket, budget, detailsJson, status }. actorRole/actorName
    // stamp both enteredBy* and lastEditedBy* — this is the row's first
    // touch, so "entered by" and "last edited by" start out identical.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'channel-planning'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      // Round 132x (2026-08-10) — now a thin wrapper around
      // insertChannelPlanningRow(), the same function the bulk uploader
      // uses, so single-entry and bulk creation can never drift apart.
      try {
        const { entryId } = insertChannelPlanningRow({ ...body, campaignId });
        syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning(campaignId);
        return sendJson(res, 201, { entryId });
      } catch (e){
        if (e && e.status) return sendJson(res, e.status, { error: e.error });
        throw e;
      }
    }

    // PATCH /api/campaigns/:id/channel-planning/:entryId — update one entry.
    // Same cost-subfield role guard as POST (checked against the MERGED
    // detailsJson, so a client can't smuggle a cost value through by only
    // sending it once and leaving it out of later merge-updates — every
    // detailsJson value present in the final row, sent or carried over, is
    // checked). Same status-transition guard: only actorRole === 'cx_ops'
    // may move a row to 'approved'.
    if (req.method === 'PATCH' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'channel-planning'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const entryId = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM channel_planning_details WHERE id = ? AND campaignId = ?').get(entryId, campaignId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'channel planning entry not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const actorRole = body.actorRole === 'cx_ops' ? 'cx_ops' : 'client';
      const actorName = typeof body.actorName === 'string' ? body.actorName : '';
      const existingDetails = existing.detailsJson ? JSON.parse(existing.detailsJson) : {};
      const incomingDetails = (body.detailsJson && typeof body.detailsJson === 'object') ? body.detailsJson : {};
      const mergedDetails = { ...existingDetails, ...incomingDetails };
      const presentCostFields = channelPlanningCostFieldsPresent(mergedDetails);
      const changedCostFields = presentCostFields.filter(k => JSON.stringify(existingDetails[k]) !== JSON.stringify(mergedDetails[k]));
      if (actorRole !== 'cx_ops' && changedCostFields.length){
        return sendJson(res, 403, { error: `Only CX Experiences Media Ops may edit vendor cost fields (${changedCostFields.join(', ')}) — clients can view but not edit vendor rate/cost economics.` });
      }
      let status = body.status !== undefined ? body.status : existing.status;
      if (status === 'approved' && existing.status !== 'approved' && actorRole !== 'cx_ops'){
        return sendJson(res, 403, { error: 'Only CX Experiences Media Ops may set status to approved.' });
      }
      const merged = {
        allocationId: body.allocationId !== undefined ? body.allocationId : existing.allocationId,
        channel: body.channel !== undefined ? body.channel : existing.channel,
        partner: body.partner !== undefined ? body.partner : existing.partner,
        audience: body.audience !== undefined ? body.audience : existing.audience,
        buyType: body.buyType !== undefined ? body.buyType : existing.buyType,
        mediaType: body.mediaType !== undefined ? body.mediaType : existing.mediaType,
        impressions: body.impressions !== undefined ? body.impressions : existing.impressions,
        dropDate: body.dropDate !== undefined ? body.dropDate : existing.dropDate,
        hitDate: body.hitDate !== undefined ? body.hitDate : existing.hitDate,
        endDate: body.endDate !== undefined ? body.endDate : existing.endDate,
        productYear: body.productYear !== undefined ? body.productYear : existing.productYear,
        productGroup: body.productGroup !== undefined ? body.productGroup : existing.productGroup,
        creativeMarket: body.creativeMarket !== undefined ? body.creativeMarket : existing.creativeMarket,
        budget: body.budget !== undefined ? body.budget : existing.budget,
        status
      };
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`UPDATE channel_planning_details SET
          allocationId = ?, channel = ?, partner = ?, audience = ?, buyType = ?, mediaType = ?, impressions = ?,
          dropDate = ?, hitDate = ?, endDate = ?, productYear = ?, productGroup = ?, creativeMarket = ?, budget = ?,
          detailsJson = ?, status = ?, lastEditedByRole = ?, lastEditedByName = ?, updatedAt = ?
        WHERE id = ?`
      ).run(
        merged.allocationId, merged.channel, merged.partner, merged.audience, merged.buyType, merged.mediaType, merged.impressions,
        merged.dropDate, merged.hitDate, merged.endDate, merged.productYear, merged.productGroup, merged.creativeMarket, merged.budget,
        JSON.stringify(mergedDetails), merged.status, actorRole, actorName, now,
        entryId
      );
      // Round 132x (2026-08-10) — an edit can change productGroup/
      // creativeMarket on an existing row, so the campaign-level rollup
      // needs the same resync a fresh insert gets.
      syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning(campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { updatedAt: now });
    }

    // DELETE /api/campaigns/:id/channel-planning/:entryId
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'channel-planning'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const entryId = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM channel_planning_details WHERE id = ? AND campaignId = ?').get(entryId, campaignId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'channel planning entry not found' });
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM channel_planning_details WHERE id = ?').run(entryId);
      // Round 132x (2026-08-10) — recompute off whatever rows remain (see
      // syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning's own comment
      // for why this is a no-op, not a blank-out, when none remain).
      syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning(campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:accountId/channel-planning/bulk — round 132x
    // (2026-08-10), per direct instruction: the Manage Account bulk
    // uploader. Body: { actorRole ('cx_ops'|'client'), actorName,
    // rows: [{ campaignId, channel, partner, audience, buyType, mediaType,
    // impressions, dropDate, hitDate, endDate, productYear, productGroup,
    // creativeMarket, budget, allocationId, detailsJson, status }, ...] }.
    // actorRole/actorName are set once for the whole batch (whoever is
    // uploading) and applied to every row, overriding anything a row
    // itself might carry — a bulk CSV column can't spoof a different
    // actor per line. Each row is inserted through the exact same
    // insertChannelPlanningRow() the single-entry POST above now also
    // calls, so the cost-field role guard and the draft/submitted/
    // approved gate apply identically here — a client-uploaded CSV with a
    // Unit Cost column, or a status of "approved", fails those specific
    // rows with the same 403 message a single manual entry would get, not
    // a different bulk-only rule.
    //
    // Partial success by design: one bad row (an unmatched campaignId, a
    // missing channel, a client trying to set a cost field) does not fail
    // the batch — every row is attempted independently and the response
    // reports per-row ok/error so the uploader can show exactly which
    // rows need fixing and which already succeeded, rather than an
    // all-or-nothing transaction that would make a 500-row file with one
    // typo entirely unusable.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'channel-planning' && parts[4] === 'bulk'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const rows = Array.isArray(body.rows) ? body.rows : [];
      if (!rows.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'rows must be a non-empty array' });
      if (rows.length > 2000) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'bulk upload is capped at 2000 rows per request — split into smaller batches' });
      const actorRole = body.actorRole === 'cx_ops' ? 'cx_ops' : 'client';
      const actorName = typeof body.actorName === 'string' ? body.actorName : '';
      // Cache campaign->accountId lookups — a real upload can have many
      // rows against the same handful of campaigns, no need to re-query.
      const campaignAccountCache = new Map();
      const results = [];
      const touchedCampaignIds = new Set();
      rows.forEach((row, index) => {
        try {
          if (!row || !row.campaignId) throw { status: 400, error: 'campaignId is required' };
          let rowAccountId = campaignAccountCache.get(row.campaignId);
          if (rowAccountId === undefined){
            const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(row.campaignId);
            rowAccountId = campaign ? campaign.accountId : null;
            campaignAccountCache.set(row.campaignId, rowAccountId);
          }
          if (rowAccountId === null) throw { status: 404, error: `campaign not found: ${row.campaignId}` };
          if (rowAccountId !== accountId) throw { status: 403, error: `campaign ${row.campaignId} does not belong to this account` };
          const { entryId, campaignId } = insertChannelPlanningRow({ ...row, actorRole, actorName });
          touchedCampaignIds.add(campaignId);
          results.push({ index, ok: true, entryId, campaignId });
        } catch (e){
          results.push({ index, ok: false, campaignId: row && row.campaignId, error: (e && e.error) || 'unexpected error inserting this row' });
        }
      });
      touchedCampaignIds.forEach(id => syncCampaignProductCreativeGroupsFromChannelPlanning(id));
      const insertedCount = results.filter(r => r.ok).length;
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        insertedCount, failedCount: results.length - insertedCount,
        results, campaignsSynced: [...touchedCampaignIds]
      });
    }

    // GET /api/campaigns/:id/mmm-line-items — round 89: this campaign's real
    // per-MMM-category actuals (Spend/Reach/Impressions), plus the current
    // computed totals stored on the campaign row itself.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'mmm-line-items'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        lineItems: getCampaignMmmLineItems(campaignId),
        actualSpend: campaign.actualSpend, actualReach: campaign.actualReach, actualImpressions: campaign.actualImpressions
      });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/mmm-line-items — round 89, per direct
    // instruction: "during the campaign creation process users will be
    // asked to create a line item per category" with real Spend/Reach/
    // Impressions. Upsert-whole-set (delete + reinsert), same convention as
    // Media Plan's allocation save — a category left out of the request, or
    // sent with all three fields empty, simply has no row (never a padded
    // zero row). Every category must be one of the fixed 18 MMM categories;
    // this endpoint doesn't gate on the account's own MMM inclusions list,
    // since a campaign may reasonably report on a category before the
    // account has gotten to Step 1 of the MMM card. After saving, recomputes
    // and persists this campaign's actualSpend/actualReach/actualImpressions
    // as the sum of its line items — per direct instruction that total
    // impressions (and, by the same logic, spend/reach) become a calculated
    // field rather than separately hand-typed numbers.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'mmm-line-items'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const lineItems = Array.isArray(body.lineItems) ? body.lineItems : [];
      const invalid = lineItems.filter(li => !li || !MMM_CATEGORIES.includes(li.category));
      if (invalid.length){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `Unknown categor${invalid.length === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'}: ${invalid.map(li => (li && li.category) || '(missing)').join(', ')}` });
      }
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM campaign_mmm_line_items WHERE campaignId = ?').run(campaignId);
      const insert = db.prepare('INSERT INTO campaign_mmm_line_items (id, campaignId, category, spend, reach, impressions, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      lineItems.forEach(li => {
        const spend = li.spend != null && li.spend !== '' ? Number(li.spend) : null;
        const reach = li.reach != null && li.reach !== '' ? Number(li.reach) : null;
        const impressions = li.impressions != null && li.impressions !== '' ? Number(li.impressions) : null;
        if (spend == null && reach == null && impressions == null) return; // nothing reported for this category — no row
        insert.run(generateId('CLI'), campaignId, li.category, spend, reach, impressions, now);
      });
      const totals = recomputeCampaignActuals(campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { lineItems: getCampaignMmmLineItems(campaignId), ...totals });
    }

    // Shared by all three Media Plan export routes below (xlsx/csv/
    // snowflake) so they can never disagree on which rows got exported.
    // Round 127, per direct instruction ("Add options to download as
    // Excel, CSV, Google Sheet or Snowflake") — same pattern as
    // getMarketingCalendarEvents() above: one row-builder, several transports.
    function getMediaPlanExportRows(accountId){
      const plan = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY updatedAt DESC LIMIT 1').get(accountId);
      if (!plan) return { plan: null, rows: [] };
      const allocations = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ? ORDER BY stage, channel').all(plan.id);
      const rows = allocations.map(a => ({
        stage: a.stage,
        channel: a.channel,
        audienceType: a.audienceType || 'blended',
        recommendedAmount: a.recommendedAmount,
        approvedAmount: a.approvedAmount,
        cpm: a.cpm
      }));
      return { plan, rows };
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/media-plan/export.xlsx — genuine .xlsx
    // binary via xlsx_gen.py, same "shell out to openpyxl" pattern the
