    // Marketing Calendar export already established above (this sandbox's
    // npm registry blocks exceljs/xlsx/node-xlsx, so there's no in-process
    // JS writer available). Matched before the plain GET media-plan route
    // below (parts.length 5 vs 4), same ordering discipline as that route.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'media-plan' && parts[4] === 'export.xlsx'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const { plan, rows } = getMediaPlanExportRows(accountId);
      if (!plan) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no media plan on this account yet' });
      const headers = ['Stage', 'Channel', 'Audience Type', 'Recommended Amount', 'Approved Amount', 'Est. CPM'];
      const sheetRows = rows.map(r => [r.stage, r.channel, r.audienceType, r.recommendedAmount, r.approvedAmount, r.cpm]);
      try {
        const b64 = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          const proc = spawn('python3', [path.join(__dirname, 'xlsx_gen.py')]);
          let out = '', errOut = '';
          proc.stdout.on('data', c => { out += c; });
          proc.stderr.on('data', c => { errOut += c; });
          proc.on('error', reject);
          proc.on('close', code => {
            if (code !== 0) return reject(new Error(errOut || `xlsx_gen.py exited ${code}`));
            resolve(out);
          });
          proc.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ sheetName: `Media Plan ${plan.year}`, headers, rows: sheetRows }));
          proc.stdin.end();
        });
        const buf = Buffer.from(b64, 'base64');
        res.writeHead(200, {
          'Content-Type': 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
          'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="media-plan-${plan.year}.xlsx"`,
          'Content-Length': buf.length,
          'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
        });
        return res.end(buf);
      } catch (e){
        return sendJson(res, 500, { error: 'xlsx export failed: ' + e.message });
      }
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:accountId/media-plan/export.csv — same row set as
    // export.xlsx, RFC4180-quoted UTF-8 CSV. This is what both the "Export
    // CSV for Google Sheets" and the "Send to Snowflake" affordances below
    // download — same honest pattern as the Marketing Calendar export: no
    // Sheets API/OAuth and no live Snowflake push integration exist in this
    // prototype (the Snowflake connector this product has scoped is
    // read-only, client-warehouse-into-product, per
    // cxmedia-data-integration-architecture.md — not a push-out path), so
    // the CSV download IS the deliverable in both cases, not a workaround.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'media-plan' && parts[4] === 'export.csv'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const { plan, rows } = getMediaPlanExportRows(accountId);
      if (!plan) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no media plan on this account yet' });
      const headers = ['Stage', 'Channel', 'Audience Type', 'Recommended Amount', 'Approved Amount', 'Est. CPM'];
      const csvCell = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const lines = [headers.map(csvCell).join(',')];
      rows.forEach(r => {
        lines.push([r.stage, r.channel, r.audienceType, r.recommendedAmount === null ? '' : r.recommendedAmount, r.approvedAmount === null ? '' : r.approvedAmount, r.cpm === null ? '' : r.cpm].map(csvCell).join(','));
      });
      const csv = '﻿' + lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n'; // BOM so Excel/Sheets reliably detect UTF-8
      res.writeHead(200, {
        'Content-Type': 'text/csv; charset=utf-8',
        'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="media-plan-${plan.year}.csv"`,
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*'
      });
      return res.end(csv);
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/media-plan — round 53, extended round 132bx
    // follow-on (multi-year). One row per (account, year) now that
    // media_plans.year is a real key rather than a decorative field — an
    // account can have a saved plan for several years at once (past actuals
    // kept on file, a current-year working plan, future-year forecasts).
    // ?year=YYYY selects a specific year's plan; omitted, this keeps the
    // pre-multi-year behavior (most recently updated plan on file) so any
    // caller that hasn't been updated to pass a year yet still works.
    // Returns { plan: null } if the account has no plan for the requested
    // year (or no plan at all).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'media-plan'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const requestedYearRaw = url.searchParams.get('year');
      const requestedYear = requestedYearRaw ? parseInt(requestedYearRaw, 10) : null;
      const plan = requestedYear
        ? db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? AND year = ? ORDER BY updatedAt DESC LIMIT 1').get(accountId, requestedYear)
        : db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY updatedAt DESC LIMIT 1').get(accountId);
      if (!plan) return sendJson(res, 200, { plan: null, allocations: [] });
      const allocations = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ?').all(plan.id);
      // Round 54 — nest each allocation's period rows (if any) onto it, so
      // the front end gets one shape back rather than a second flat list it
      // has to join itself.
      const getPeriods = db.prepare('SELECT id, label, approvedAmount FROM media_plan_periods WHERE allocationId = ?');
      allocations.forEach(a => { a.periods = getPeriods.all(a.id); });
      // Round 132bx follow-on — unpack the JSON columns back into the shape
      // the frontend's state.mediaPlan expects; parse failures fall back to
      // an empty array/default rather than a 500 on a corrupted/legacy row.
      try { plan.nonWorkingMedia = plan.nonWorkingMediaJson ? JSON.parse(plan.nonWorkingMediaJson) : []; } catch (e) { plan.nonWorkingMedia = []; }
      try { plan.monthlyPct = plan.monthlyPctJson ? JSON.parse(plan.monthlyPctJson) : null; } catch (e) { plan.monthlyPct = null; }
      delete plan.nonWorkingMediaJson; delete plan.monthlyPctJson;
      return sendJson(res, 200, { plan, allocations });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/media-plan — round 53, extended round 132bx
    // follow-on (multi-year). Upsert-whole-plan: creates the plan row for
    // this (account, year) if none exists yet, otherwise updates that
    // year's plan in place, and always replaces ITS OWN allocations set
    // wholesale (delete + reinsert) — simplest correct behavior for a
    // single "Save plan" button, matching the front end's one-shot save
    // flow. Before multi-year support this looked up the account's single
    // most-recently-updated plan regardless of year, which — now that the
    // front end can save several different years for the same account —
    // would have silently overwritten whichever year was last touched
    // instead of the year actually being saved. Now looked up by
    // (accountId, year) so saving 2027's plan can never clobber 2026's.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'media-plan'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const targetYear = body.year || new Date().getFullYear();
      let plan = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? AND year = ? ORDER BY updatedAt DESC LIMIT 1').get(accountId, targetYear);
      let planId;
      const granularity = body.trancheGranularity === 'monthly' ? 'monthly' : 'quarterly';
      // Round 132bx follow-on — grossRevenue/nonWorkingMedia/monthlyMode/
      // monthlyPct now persisted too (see the columns' own comment above);
      // nonWorkingMedia and monthlyPct stored as JSON text.
      const nonWorkingMediaJson = JSON.stringify(Array.isArray(body.nonWorkingMedia) ? body.nonWorkingMedia : []);
      const monthlyMode = body.monthlyMode === 'manual' ? 'manual' : 'flat';
      const monthlyPctJson = body.monthlyPct && typeof body.monthlyPct === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(body.monthlyPct) : null;
      // Round 132c13 — marketingBudgetPct/tradeMarketingPct/
      // tradeMarketingBudget, same fall-back-to-existing-value pattern as
      // grossRevenue just above (a field the frontend didn't send this
      // save shouldn't silently null out what was there).
      if (plan){
        planId = plan.id;
        db.prepare('UPDATE media_plans SET year = ?, totalBudget = ?, usesTranches = ?, trancheGranularity = ?, grossRevenue = ?, nonWorkingMediaJson = ?, monthlyMode = ?, monthlyPctJson = ?, marketingBudgetPct = ?, tradeMarketingPct = ?, tradeMarketingBudget = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
          .run(body.year || plan.year, typeof body.totalBudget === 'number' ? body.totalBudget : plan.totalBudget, body.usesTranches ? 1 : 0, granularity, typeof body.grossRevenue === 'number' ? body.grossRevenue : plan.grossRevenue, nonWorkingMediaJson, monthlyMode, monthlyPctJson, typeof body.marketingBudgetPct === 'number' ? body.marketingBudgetPct : plan.marketingBudgetPct, typeof body.tradeMarketingPct === 'number' ? body.tradeMarketingPct : plan.tradeMarketingPct, typeof body.tradeMarketingBudget === 'number' ? body.tradeMarketingBudget : plan.tradeMarketingBudget, now, planId);
      } else {
        planId = generateId('PLAN');
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO media_plans (id, accountId, year, totalBudget, usesTranches, trancheGranularity, grossRevenue, nonWorkingMediaJson, monthlyMode, monthlyPctJson, marketingBudgetPct, tradeMarketingPct, tradeMarketingBudget, createdAt, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)')
          .run(planId, accountId, body.year || new Date().getFullYear(), typeof body.totalBudget === 'number' ? body.totalBudget : null, body.usesTranches ? 1 : 0, granularity, typeof body.grossRevenue === 'number' ? body.grossRevenue : null, nonWorkingMediaJson, monthlyMode, monthlyPctJson, typeof body.marketingBudgetPct === 'number' ? body.marketingBudgetPct : null, typeof body.tradeMarketingPct === 'number' ? body.tradeMarketingPct : null, typeof body.tradeMarketingBudget === 'number' ? body.tradeMarketingBudget : null, now, now);
      }
      // Round 54 — delete this plan's existing period rows before its
      // allocations (periods reference allocationId, and allocation ids are
      // re-inserted fresh below even when they reuse the same string, so
      // this avoids ever leaving an orphaned period row on file).
      const existingAllocIds = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ?').all(planId).map(r => r.id);
      const deletePeriods = db.prepare('DELETE FROM media_plan_periods WHERE allocationId = ?');
      existingAllocIds.forEach(id => deletePeriods.run(id));
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ?').run(planId);
      const insertAlloc = db.prepare('INSERT INTO media_plan_allocations (id, mediaPlanId, stage, channel, recommendedAmount, approvedAmount, cpm, audienceType, playbookAudience, playbookCreative, playbookKpi) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)');
      const insertPeriod = db.prepare('INSERT INTO media_plan_periods (id, allocationId, label, approvedAmount) VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
      (body.allocations || []).forEach(a => {
        const allocId = a.id || generateId('ALLOC');
        insertAlloc.run(allocId, planId, a.stage, a.channel, typeof a.recommendedAmount === 'number' ? a.recommendedAmount : null, typeof a.approvedAmount === 'number' ? a.approvedAmount : null, typeof a.cpm === 'number' ? a.cpm : null, a.audienceType || 'blended', (a.playbookAudience || '').trim() || null, (a.playbookCreative || '').trim() || null, (a.playbookKpi || '').trim() || null);
        (a.periods || []).forEach(p => {
          insertPeriod.run(generateId('PERIOD'), allocId, p.label, typeof p.approvedAmount === 'number' ? p.approvedAmount : null);
        });
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { planId });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/media-plan/history — round 132bx follow-on
    // (multi-year), per direct instruction: "add opportunities to multiple
    // years of history and forecasts... should make our product smarter and
    // more relevant to the client when making recommendations." Returns
    // every saved (accountId, year) plan on file — not windowed to any
    // particular range, since a small prototype account may only have 1-2
    // years saved and a window would just return mostly nulls; the front
    // end applies its own 3-back/2-forward display window on top of
    // whatever this returns. Each entry includes totalBudget and a flat
    // per-channel approvedAmount breakdown (summed across stages, since a
    // channel can appear in more than one funnel stage) — enough for a
    // trend chart, a YoY comparison, and channel-share-weighted
    // recommendations without a second round-trip per year.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'media-plan' && parts[4] === 'history'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const plans = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY year ASC').all(accountId);
      const getAllocs = db.prepare('SELECT stage, channel, approvedAmount FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ?');
      const years = plans.map(plan => {
        const allocs = getAllocs.all(plan.id);
        const byChannel = {};
        allocs.forEach(a => {
          if (typeof a.approvedAmount !== 'number') return;
          byChannel[a.channel] = (byChannel[a.channel] || 0) + a.approvedAmount;
        });
        return {
          year: plan.year,
          totalBudget: plan.totalBudget || 0,
          grossRevenue: plan.grossRevenue || 0,
          updatedAt: plan.updatedAt,
          channelTotals: byChannel
        };
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { years });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/projects — create a project under a campaign
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'projects'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.projectType) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'projectType is required' });
      const projectId = generateId('PRJ');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO projects (id, campaignId, projectType, status, masterContent, derivatives, createdAt, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)'
      ).run(projectId, campaignId, body.projectType, 'draft', '', null, now, now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { projectId });
    }

    // ---------- Round 64 — Creative Jobs (grouping & prioritizing creative
    // requests) ----------

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/creative-jobs — send a creative request (e.g.
    // a Print or OOH spec selection) to the creative team under this
    // campaign.
    //
    // Round 74 — per direct instruction: "one job id per campaign id and
    // asset class... should be created vs a job id for every size." channel
    // IS the asset class (Print / Out-of-Home today; Digital/Video/Social
    // etc. once those pickers exist) — so this is now an UPSERT keyed on
    // (campaignId, channel), not an unconditional INSERT: every size sent
    // for the same campaign+channel lands as another entry in that one
    // job's specsJson array, rather than spawning a new Job ID per size.
    // A channel's job is only reopened for merging while it's still active
    // (status !== 'Complete') — once a channel's job is marked Complete,
    // the next spec sent for that channel starts a fresh job (a new round
    // of work), rather than silently reopening finished, delivered work.
    // No retroactive migration of pre-round-74 one-job-per-size data — this
    // only changes how NEW requests are grouped.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'creative-jobs'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId, campaignCode FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.channel) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'channel is required' });
      const priority = CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER.hasOwnProperty(body.priority) ? body.priority : 'Medium';
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const newSpec = {
        deliveryPlatform: body.deliveryPlatform || null, sizeOrLength: body.sizeOrLength || null,
        aspect: body.aspect || null, specSummary: body.specSummary || null,
        specsJson: body.specsJson || null, addedAt: now
      };
      // Request-form due/delivery dates (YYYY-MM-DD, from <input type="date">
      // on print-specs.html / ooh-specs.html) — both optional at request
      // time, since not every asset has a hard deadline yet.
      const reqDueDate = body.dueDate || null;
      const reqDeliveryDate = body.deliveryDate || null;
      // Earlier-wins merge: when two sizes for the same campaign+channel
      // carry different dates, the tighter one is the one the whole job
      // actually has to hit. Plain string compare is safe for YYYY-MM-DD.
      const earlierDate = (a, b) => (!a ? b : !b ? a : (a < b ? a : b));

      const existing = db.prepare(
        `SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE campaignId = ? AND channel = ? AND status != 'Complete' ORDER BY createdAt DESC LIMIT 1`
      ).get(campaignId, body.channel);

      if (existing){
        let specs = [];
        try { specs = existing.specsJson ? JSON.parse(existing.specsJson) : []; } catch (e){ specs = []; }
        if (!Array.isArray(specs)) specs = specs ? [specs] : []; // pre-round-74 rows stored a single object
        specs.push(newSpec);
        const specSummary = specs.length > 1
          ? `${specs.length} sizes: ${specs.map(s => s.specSummary || s.sizeOrLength || '—').join('; ')}`
          : (specs[0].specSummary || null);
        // Upgrading priority (never downgrading) — a new Urgent size on an
        // otherwise Medium job should surface the whole job as Urgent.
        const mergedPriority = (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[priority] ?? 2) < (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[existing.priority] ?? 2)
          ? priority : existing.priority;
        const mergedDueDate = earlierDate(existing.dueDate, reqDueDate);
        const mergedDeliveryDate = earlierDate(existing.deliveryDate, reqDeliveryDate);
        db.prepare('UPDATE creative_jobs SET specsJson = ?, specSummary = ?, priority = ?, dueDate = ?, deliveryDate = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
          .run(JSON.stringify(specs), specSummary, mergedPriority, mergedDueDate, mergedDeliveryDate, now, existing.id);
        return sendJson(res, 200, { jobId: existing.id, priority: mergedPriority, status: existing.status, createdAt: existing.createdAt, merged: true, specCount: specs.length, dueDate: mergedDueDate, deliveryDate: mergedDeliveryDate });
      }

      const jobId = generateId('JOB');
      const specs = [newSpec];
      // Round 132c17 — Job ID = campaign's Campaign Code + a 2-digit
      // sequential number starting at 01, unique within that campaign (per
      // direct instruction). Falls back to counting existing jobs by
      // campaignId (not campaignCode) so numbering still works for campaigns
      // that don't have a Campaign Code yet (no partnerCode on file) — the
      // internal jobId (generateId('JOB')) stays the real primary key
      // regardless; jobCode is the human-facing label.
      const jobCountRow = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM creative_jobs WHERE campaignId = ?').get(campaignId);
      const jobNumber = (jobCountRow ? jobCountRow.n : 0) + 1;
      const jobCode = `${campaign.campaignCode || campaignId}-${String(jobNumber).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const skill = mapChannelToSkill(body.channel);
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO creative_jobs
        (id, accountId, campaignId, channel, deliveryPlatform, sizeOrLength, aspect, specSummary, specsJson, priority, status, requestedBy, notes, dueDate, deliveryDate, jobNumber, jobCode, skill, createdAt, updatedAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(
          jobId, campaign.accountId, campaignId, body.channel, body.deliveryPlatform || null,
          body.sizeOrLength || null, body.aspect || null, body.specSummary || null,
          JSON.stringify(specs),
          priority, 'Pending', body.requestedBy || null, body.notes || null, reqDueDate, reqDeliveryDate,
          jobNumber, jobCode, skill, now, now
        );
      return sendJson(res, 201, { jobId, jobCode, skill, priority, status: 'Pending', createdAt: now, merged: false, specCount: 1, dueDate: reqDueDate, deliveryDate: reqDeliveryDate });
    }

    // GET /api/campaigns/:id/creative-jobs — every creative job requested
    // under this campaign, sorted by priority (Urgent → Low) then oldest
    // first within a priority, matching how a creative team would actually
    // triage a queue for one campaign.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'creative-jobs'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const jobs = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE campaignId = ?').all(campaignId)
        .sort((a, b) => (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[a.priority] ?? 2) - (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[b.priority] ?? 2) || a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { jobs });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/creative-jobs — every creative job across every
    // campaign on this account, each row carrying its campaign's name/
    // objective so a creative team's account-wide queue can group by
    // campaign and still sort the whole list by priority.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'creative-jobs'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      // Round 132aa — campaignApprovedAssetJobIds rides along so this
      // account-wide rollup can honor the same Performance Marketing
      // asset-approval gate the single-campaign view already enforces
      // client-side, without a second round-trip per job's campaign.
      const jobs = db.prepare(
        `SELECT creative_jobs.*, campaigns.name AS campaignName, campaigns.objective AS campaignObjective, campaigns.approvedAssetJobIds AS campaignApprovedAssetJobIds
         FROM creative_jobs JOIN campaigns ON campaigns.id = creative_jobs.campaignId
         WHERE creative_jobs.accountId = ?`
      ).all(accountId)
        .sort((a, b) => (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[a.priority] ?? 2) - (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER[b.priority] ?? 2) || a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { jobs });
    }

    // PATCH /api/creative-jobs/:id — update priority/status/notes on an
    // existing request (e.g. the creative team bumping priority, or marking
    // a job In Progress / Delivered). Ownership resolved through
    // creative_jobs' own accountId column, same requireAccount() gate as
    // every other account-scoped route.
    if (req.method === 'PATCH' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const current = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      const priority = body.priority !== undefined
        ? (CREATIVE_JOB_PRIORITY_ORDER.hasOwnProperty(body.priority) ? body.priority : current.priority)
        : current.priority;
      // Round 74 — status is now a fixed 4-value vocabulary (this task grid
      // is a designer-facing view, not a free-text field): Pending → In
      // Progress → Pending Approval → Complete.
      const CREATIVE_JOB_STATUSES = ['Pending', 'In Progress', 'Pending Approval', 'Complete'];
      let status = body.status !== undefined ? body.status : current.status;
      if (body.status !== undefined && !CREATIVE_JOB_STATUSES.includes(status)){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `status must be one of: ${CREATIVE_JOB_STATUSES.join(', ')}` });
      }
      const notes = body.notes !== undefined ? body.notes : current.notes;
      // Round 74 — Due Date / Delivery Date ("date assets sent to
      // publisher"), both manually set — nothing in this build computes
      // them automatically.
      const dueDate = body.dueDate !== undefined ? body.dueDate : current.dueDate;
      const deliveryDate = body.deliveryDate !== undefined ? body.deliveryDate : current.deliveryDate;
      // Round 68 — copy workspace fields, same merge-update convention.
      const workingCopy = body.workingCopy !== undefined ? body.workingCopy : current.workingCopy;
      const copyApproved = body.copyApproved !== undefined ? (body.copyApproved ? 1 : 0) : current.copyApproved;
      // Direct instruction: "copy must be approved before going to creative
      // next step." 'Pending' (round 74 — was 'Requested') is the job's
      // starting status (copy/spec review); anything past that is the
      // creative team actually executing against it, so that's the gate
      // point — reject the move if the copy this job would advance with
      // isn't approved (checking the resulting value, not just `current`,
      // so approving copy and advancing status in the same request still
      // works).
      if (status !== current.status && status !== 'Pending' && !copyApproved){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'Copy must be approved before this job can move to its next step.' });
      }
      const copyApprovedAt = body.copyApproved !== undefined
        ? (body.copyApproved ? new Date().toISOString() : null)
        : current.copyApprovedAt;
      // Round 73 — Creative Asset approval, a SEPARATE gate from Copy
      // approval above (confirmed via direct follow-up: copy and final
      // creative-asset sign-off are different checkpoints). Same "check the
      // resulting value" pattern as the copy gate — reject only if this
      // request would actually leave assetApproved true without copyApproved
      // also true, so a request that sets both at once still works.
      const assetApproved = body.assetApproved !== undefined ? (body.assetApproved ? 1 : 0) : current.assetApproved;
      if (assetApproved && !copyApproved){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'Copy must be approved before this creative asset can be approved.' });
      }
      const assetApprovedAt = body.assetApproved !== undefined
        ? (body.assetApproved ? new Date().toISOString() : null)
        : current.assetApprovedAt;
      // Round 74 — approving the creative asset finishes the job, so it
      // auto-advances status to 'Complete' unless this same request already
      // specified a different status explicitly (an explicit status always
      // wins over this default).
      if (body.assetApproved === true && body.status === undefined){
        status = 'Complete';
      }
      const trackingUrl = body.trackingUrl !== undefined ? body.trackingUrl : current.trackingUrl;
      // Round 76 — per-job actuals (this specific placement's spend/reach/
      // response), same manual entry pattern as the campaign-level actuals
      // in the Overview card. Additive — a job with no actuals entered just
      // reads as null, same as a campaign that never reached Analysis.
      const actualSpend = body.actualSpend !== undefined ? body.actualSpend : current.actualSpend;
      const actualImpressions = body.actualImpressions !== undefined ? body.actualImpressions : current.actualImpressions;
      const actualConversions = body.actualConversions !== undefined ? body.actualConversions : current.actualConversions;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`UPDATE creative_jobs SET priority = ?, status = ?, notes = ?, dueDate = ?, deliveryDate = ?, workingCopy = ?, copyApproved = ?, copyApprovedAt = ?,
        assetApproved = ?, assetApprovedAt = ?, trackingUrl = ?, actualSpend = ?, actualImpressions = ?, actualConversions = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?`)
        .run(priority, status, notes, dueDate, deliveryDate, workingCopy, copyApproved, copyApprovedAt, assetApproved, assetApprovedAt, trackingUrl, actualSpend, actualImpressions, actualConversions, now, jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { updatedAt: now, status, copyApproved: !!copyApproved, assetApproved: !!assetApproved, trackingUrl, dueDate, deliveryDate, actualSpend, actualImpressions, actualConversions });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/cx-relevance-score — round 132c17, per
    // direct instruction: "All creative must come back for final CX
    // Relevance Scoring." A separate gate from status (which stays the
    // fixed round-74 vocabulary, untouched) — a job can sit at status
    // 'Complete' with cxRelevanceScore still null, and the front end's
    // creative-jobs list flags that as "Awaiting CX Relevance Scoring"
    // rather than treating Complete alone as fully done. Scoring is only
    // meaningful once the job is actually Complete, so this rejects earlier
    // statuses rather than silently accepting a score for unfinished work.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'cx-relevance-score'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      if (existing.status !== 'Complete'){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'This job must be Complete before it can receive a CX Relevance Score.' });
      }
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const score = typeof body.cxRelevanceScore === 'number' ? body.cxRelevanceScore : null;
      if (score === null || score < 0 || score > 100){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'cxRelevanceScore must be a number 0-100.' });
      }
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_jobs SET cxRelevanceScore = ?, cxRelevanceScoredAt = ?, cxRelevanceNotes = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(score, now, body.cxRelevanceNotes || null, now, jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { cxRelevanceScore: score, cxRelevanceScoredAt: now });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/score — 2026-08-22, pre-decision draft
    // scoring (roadmap item 2). Scores whatever is CURRENTLY in
    // creative_jobs.workingCopy (or an explicit { copy } in the body, so
    // the frontend can score text still sitting in the textarea before it's
    // even been saved) against the job's own campaign context and the
    // account's brand voice guide. Persists the result on the job so a
    // reload shows the last score without re-scoring, and returns it
    // directly so the copy workspace can render it immediately.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'score'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const job = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!job) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, job.accountId)) return;
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(job.campaignId);
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(job.accountId);
      if (!campaign || !account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign or account not found for this job' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const copyText = typeof body.copy === 'string' ? body.copy : (job.workingCopy || '');
      const result = await scoreDraftCopy(copyText, campaign, account);
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`UPDATE creative_jobs SET draftRelevanceScore = ?, draftComplianceScore = ?, draftScoreNote = ?, draftComplianceFlags = ?, draftScoredAt = ? WHERE id = ?`)
        .run(result.relevanceScore, result.complianceScore, result.note || null, JSON.stringify(result.flags || []), now, jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        relevanceScore: result.relevanceScore, complianceScore: result.complianceScore,
        note: result.note || null, flags: result.flags || [], mode: result.mode, scoredAt: now
      });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/copy-decision — 2026-08-22, structured
    // approve/reject-with-reason capture (CX & Copywriting vision doc,
    // roadmap item 1). Body: { approved: boolean, reason?: string }. reason
    // is required when approved is false — an unlabeled rejection carries
    // no training signal, which is exactly the gap this endpoint exists to
    // close. Every call appends a permanent row to creative_job_decisions
    // (never overwritten, unlike creative_jobs.copyApproved which only
    // tracks the current state) and then applies the SAME copyApproved/
    // copyApprovedAt update the existing PATCH endpoint makes, so every
    // other gate downstream of copy approval (status advance, assetApproved)
    // keeps working unchanged. A rejection after a prior approval also
    // clears assetApproved — an asset can't stay approved once the copy
    // under it has been sent back.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'copy-decision'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const session = authenticate(req);
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (typeof body.approved !== 'boolean'){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'approved (boolean) is required' });
      }
      const reason = typeof body.reason === 'string' ? body.reason.trim() : '';
      if (!body.approved && !reason){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'reason is required when rejecting copy — this is what the next draft learns from' });
      }
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const decisionId = generateId('CJD');
      const decidedBy = session ? (session.memberId || `${session.accountId}:admin`) : null;
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO creative_job_decisions (id, jobId, accountId, approved, reason, decidedBy, decidedAt, createdAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(decisionId, jobId, existing.accountId, body.approved ? 1 : 0, reason || null, decidedBy, now, now);
      const copyApproved = body.approved ? 1 : 0;
      const copyApprovedAt = body.approved ? now : null;
      // Rejecting after a prior asset approval retracts it — see the same
      // "copy must be approved before the asset can be" gate in the PATCH
      // handler above; a rejection is the inverse of that gate firing.
      const assetApproved = body.approved ? existing.assetApproved : 0;
      const assetApprovedAt = body.approved ? existing.assetApprovedAt : null;
      db.prepare(`UPDATE creative_jobs SET copyApproved = ?, copyApprovedAt = ?, assetApproved = ?, assetApprovedAt = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?`)
        .run(copyApproved, copyApprovedAt, assetApproved, assetApprovedAt, now, jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        decisionId, approved: !!body.approved, reason: reason || null, decidedAt: now,
        copyApproved: !!copyApproved, copyApprovedAt, assetApproved: !!assetApproved
      });
    }

    // GET /api/creative-jobs/:id/copy-decisions — the full decision history
    // for a job, oldest first, so the copy workspace can show not just
    // "approved" but the trail of what was rejected and why along the way.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'copy-decisions'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const decisions = db.prepare('SELECT id, approved, reason, decidedBy, decidedAt FROM creative_job_decisions WHERE jobId = ? ORDER BY decidedAt ASC').all(jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { decisions: decisions.map(d => ({ ...d, approved: !!d.approved })) });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/interview — 2026-08-22, the multi-
    // candidate "interview" panel (roadmap item 4, last on the CX &
    // Copywriting vision doc's build list). Runs runCandidateInterview()
    // (see its comment above for the honest single-vendor/multi-angle
    // scope) against the job's own campaign + account, logs the full panel
    // result (including vendor/model) as a permanent row, and returns a
    // REDACTED copy for the copy workspace to render side-by-side — see
    // redactCandidatesForClient above. Never mutates workingCopy itself —
    // see the /select endpoint below for the explicit, one-click apply step.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'interview'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const job = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!job) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, job.accountId)) return;
      const session = authenticate(req);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(job.campaignId);
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(job.accountId);
      if (!campaign || !account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign or account not found for this job' });
      const result = await runCandidateInterview(campaign, account);
      if (!result.available){
        return sendJson(res, 200, { available: false, note: result.note, interviewId: null, recommendedKey: null, candidates: [] });
      }
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const interviewId = generateId('CJI');
      const requestedBy = session ? (session.memberId || `${session.accountId}:admin`) : null;
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO creative_job_interviews (id, jobId, accountId, requestedBy, candidatesJson, productType, createdAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(interviewId, jobId, job.accountId, requestedBy, JSON.stringify(result.candidates), 'copywriting', now);
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        available: true, interviewId, recommendedKey: result.recommendedKey, candidates: redactCandidatesForClient(result.candidates), createdAt: now
      });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/interview/:interviewId/select — records
    // which candidate a human actually picked and, ONLY on this explicit
    // click, applies its copy to creative_jobs.workingCopy — same
    // "suggestion, never a silent auto-fill" discipline as the campaign
    // smart-defaults endpoint (roadmap item 3). Body: { candidateKey }.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'interview' && parts[5] === 'select'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const interviewId = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      const job = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!job) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, job.accountId)) return;
      const interview = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_job_interviews WHERE id = ? AND jobId = ?').get(interviewId, jobId);
      if (!interview) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'interview not found for this job' });
      const session = authenticate(req);
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (typeof body.candidateKey !== 'string' || !body.candidateKey){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'candidateKey is required' });
      }
      let candidates = [];
      try { candidates = JSON.parse(interview.candidatesJson) || []; } catch (e){ candidates = []; }
      const chosen = candidates.find(c => c.key === body.candidateKey);
      if (!chosen || !chosen.copy){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'candidateKey does not match a candidate with real copy on this interview' });
      }
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const selectedBy = session ? (session.memberId || `${session.accountId}:admin`) : null;
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_job_interviews SET selectedCandidateKey = ?, selectedBy = ?, selectedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(body.candidateKey, selectedBy, now, interviewId);
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_jobs SET workingCopy = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(chosen.copy, now, jobId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { interviewId, selectedCandidateKey: body.candidateKey, selectedAt: now, workingCopy: chosen.copy });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-jobs/:id/interview/:interviewId/feedback — the
    // "no clean winner" path. Body: { note (required, free text — what they
    // don't like / what they'd change), type ('none_selected' | default, or
    // 'edit_manually'), startFromKey (optional — only meaningful with type
    // 'edit_manually': the candidate that was close enough to start from) }.
    // When startFromKey is given, this applies that candidate's copy to
    // workingCopy exactly like /select does (so it lands in the editable
    // copy box), records it as the selected candidate for reporting, AND
    // saves the note explaining what still needs to change — the two are
    // not mutually exclusive; "close enough to edit" still needs a note on
    // what's being edited. Feedback is staff-visible via the Ops Console
    // interview review (never surfaced to redactCandidatesForClient, since
    // it may reference a candidate by its real content) — this is the
    // "Feeds The Loop" half of the blind-test client slide made real: a
    // rejected or edited panel isn't a dead end, it's a record staff can
    // read to improve the next brief. NOTE: this only records the note today
    // — it does not yet feed back into generateInterviewCandidateCopy()'s
    // prompts automatically; that's the natural next step once there's
    // enough real feedback volume to design against.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'interview' && parts[5] === 'feedback'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const interviewId = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      const job = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!job) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, job.accountId)) return;
      const interview = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_job_interviews WHERE id = ? AND jobId = ?').get(interviewId, jobId);
      if (!interview) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'interview not found for this job' });
      const session = authenticate(req);
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (typeof body.note !== 'string' || !body.note.trim()){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'note is required — tell us what to change' });
      }
      const feedbackType = body.type === 'edit_manually' ? 'edit_manually' : 'none_selected';
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const actorBy = session ? (session.memberId || `${session.accountId}:admin`) : null;
      let workingCopy = null;
      if (feedbackType === 'edit_manually' && typeof body.startFromKey === 'string' && body.startFromKey){
        let candidates = [];
        try { candidates = JSON.parse(interview.candidatesJson) || []; } catch (e){ candidates = []; }
        const chosen = candidates.find(c => c.key === body.startFromKey);
        if (!chosen || !chosen.copy){
          return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'startFromKey does not match a candidate with real copy on this interview' });
        }
        db.prepare('UPDATE creative_job_interviews SET selectedCandidateKey = ?, selectedBy = ?, selectedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
          .run(body.startFromKey, actorBy, now, interviewId);
        db.prepare('UPDATE creative_jobs SET workingCopy = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(chosen.copy, now, jobId);
        workingCopy = chosen.copy;
      }
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_job_interviews SET feedbackNote = ?, feedbackType = ?, feedbackBy = ?, feedbackAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(body.note.trim(), feedbackType, actorBy, now, interviewId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { interviewId, feedbackType, feedbackNote: body.note.trim(), feedbackAt: now, workingCopy });
    }

    // GET /api/creative-jobs/:id/interviews — history of past interview
    // panels for this job, newest first, each with its full candidate set
    // and whatever was ultimately selected (if anything). Client-facing —
    // redacted through redactCandidatesForClient() above, same as
    // POST .../interview. selectedCandidateKey itself is fine to return (a
    // panel angle like "direct-response", not a model name), which is what
    // lets the copy workspace show "you picked the direct-response draft"
    // without ever naming the model behind it.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-jobs' && parts[3] === 'interviews'){
      const jobId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM creative_jobs WHERE id = ?').get(jobId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'creative job not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT id, requestedBy, candidatesJson, productType, selectedCandidateKey, selectedBy, selectedAt, feedbackNote, feedbackType, feedbackBy, feedbackAt, createdAt FROM creative_job_interviews WHERE jobId = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all(jobId);
      const interviews = rows.map(r => {
        let candidates = [];
        try { candidates = JSON.parse(r.candidatesJson) || []; } catch (e){ candidates = []; }
        return {
          id: r.id, requestedBy: r.requestedBy, productType: r.productType || 'copywriting', candidates: redactCandidatesForClient(candidates),
          selectedCandidateKey: r.selectedCandidateKey, selectedBy: r.selectedBy, selectedAt: r.selectedAt,
          feedbackNote: r.feedbackNote || null, feedbackType: r.feedbackType || null, feedbackBy: r.feedbackBy || null, feedbackAt: r.feedbackAt || null,
          createdAt: r.createdAt
        };
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { interviews });
    }

    // GET /api/ops/accounts/:id/interviews — 2026-08-22, staff-only view
    // into the interview panel's UNREDACTED results (real vendor + model
    // per candidate), per direct instruction: never reveal the actual model
    // to the client, but staff need real visibility. Same ADMIN_API_TOKEN
    // gate as GET /api/ops/integration-status — platform-staff tooling, not
    // a per-account client session, so requireAccount doesn't apply here on
    // purpose. Returns every interview run across every creative job for
    // this account, newest first, joined with the job's own channel/status
    // and the campaign name so staff don't have to cross-reference by hand.
    // Optional ?productType=copywriting query param filters to one feature
    // — added the same day the productType column was, since Todd flagged
    // this mechanism will grow more product types over time (see
    // INTERVIEW_PRODUCT_TYPES above) and the admin view needs to stay
    // legible once it's not just copywriting rows anymore. Also returns the
    // full productTypes registry so the Ops Console can render a real
    // filter dropdown instead of a hardcoded one.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'ops' && parts[2] === 'accounts' && parts[4] === 'interviews'){
      if (!ADMIN_API_TOKEN || req.headers['x-admin-token'] !== ADMIN_API_TOKEN){
        return sendJson(res, 401, { error: 'unauthorized — set ADMIN_API_TOKEN and send it as X-Admin-Token to use this endpoint' });
      }
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[3]);
      const productTypeFilter = url.searchParams.get('productType') || '';
      const rows = productTypeFilter
        ? db.prepare(`
            SELECT i.id, i.jobId, i.requestedBy, i.candidatesJson, i.productType, i.selectedCandidateKey, i.selectedBy, i.selectedAt,
                   i.feedbackNote, i.feedbackType, i.feedbackBy, i.feedbackAt, i.createdAt,
                   j.channel, j.status AS jobStatus, j.campaignId
            FROM creative_job_interviews i
            LEFT JOIN creative_jobs j ON j.id = i.jobId
            WHERE i.accountId = ? AND COALESCE(i.productType, 'copywriting') = ?
            ORDER BY i.createdAt DESC
          `).all(accountId, productTypeFilter)
        : db.prepare(`
            SELECT i.id, i.jobId, i.requestedBy, i.candidatesJson, i.productType, i.selectedCandidateKey, i.selectedBy, i.selectedAt,
                   i.feedbackNote, i.feedbackType, i.feedbackBy, i.feedbackAt, i.createdAt,
                   j.channel, j.status AS jobStatus, j.campaignId
            FROM creative_job_interviews i
            LEFT JOIN creative_jobs j ON j.id = i.jobId
            WHERE i.accountId = ?
            ORDER BY i.createdAt DESC
          `).all(accountId);
      const campaignNameCache = {};
      const interviews = rows.map(r => {
        let candidates = [];
        try { candidates = JSON.parse(r.candidatesJson) || []; } catch (e){ candidates = []; }
        if (r.campaignId && !(r.campaignId in campaignNameCache)){
          const c = db.prepare('SELECT name FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(r.campaignId);
          campaignNameCache[r.campaignId] = c ? c.name : null;
        }
        return {
          id: r.id, jobId: r.jobId, channel: r.channel, jobStatus: r.jobStatus,
          campaignId: r.campaignId, campaignName: r.campaignId ? campaignNameCache[r.campaignId] : null,
          requestedBy: r.requestedBy, productType: r.productType || 'copywriting',
          candidates, // unredacted — real vendor/model included, staff-only
          selectedCandidateKey: r.selectedCandidateKey, selectedBy: r.selectedBy, selectedAt: r.selectedAt,
          feedbackNote: r.feedbackNote || null, feedbackType: r.feedbackType || null, feedbackBy: r.feedbackBy || null, feedbackAt: r.feedbackAt || null,
          createdAt: r.createdAt
        };
      });
      const productTypes = Object.entries(INTERVIEW_PRODUCT_TYPES).map(([key, v]) => ({ key, ...v }));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { accountId, productTypes, interviews });
    }

    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/send-to-trafficking — round 73, per direct
    // instruction: "Links and Creative are sent to the trafficking team when
    // all assets are approved." Gated on EVERY creative job on this campaign
    // having both assetApproved and a trackingUrl already set — this route
    // doesn't build links or approve anything itself, it just enforces the
    // handoff can't happen until that work is actually done. Not a live
    // email/notification to a real trafficking team (none exists to
    // integrate with) — it's a genuine, persisted state change: this
    // campaign is marked ready, with a real snapshot of exactly what would
    // be handed off, same "real record, honest that there's no external
    // system on the other end" pattern as Analytics Integration's setup
    // request (round 70).
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'send-to-trafficking'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const jobs = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_jobs WHERE campaignId = ?').all(campaignId);
      if (!jobs.length){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'No creative jobs on this campaign yet — nothing to send to trafficking.' });
      }
      const notReady = jobs.filter(j => !j.assetApproved || !j.trackingUrl);
      if (notReady.length){
        return sendJson(res, 400, {
          error: `${notReady.length} of ${jobs.length} creative job(s) still need an approved asset and a tracking link before this campaign can be sent to trafficking.`,
          notReadyJobIds: notReady.map(j => j.id)
        });
      }
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const trafficPackage = jobs.map(j => ({
        jobId: j.id, channel: j.channel, deliveryPlatform: j.deliveryPlatform,
        specSummary: j.specSummary, trackingUrl: j.trackingUrl, assetApprovedAt: j.assetApprovedAt
      }));
      db.prepare('UPDATE campaigns SET trafficSentAt = ?, trafficPackageJson = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(now, JSON.stringify(trafficPackage), campaignId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { trafficSentAt: now, package: trafficPackage });
    }

    // ---------- Creative Messaging Collection (Deliverable #7) ----------
    // POST /api/campaigns/:id/creative-collections — create a new named
    // collection under this campaign. Several collections per campaign are
    // allowed by design (per cxmedia-creative-messaging-scope.md Section
    // 4a) — a campaign commonly needs more than one grouping of creative
    // requirements (e.g. a launch wave and a retargeting wave).
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'creative-collections'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.name) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'name is required' });
      const id = generateId('CMC');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO creative_collections (id, accountId, campaignId, name, createdAt, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)')
        .run(id, campaign.accountId, campaignId, body.name, now, now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, name: body.name, status: 'Draft', requirements: [], createdAt: now, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // GET /api/campaigns/:id/creative-collections — every collection under
    // this campaign, each with its requirements nested and its rolled-up
    // status computed fresh (see rollUpCollectionStatus above).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'campaigns' && parts[3] === 'creative-collections'){
      const campaignId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const campaign = db.prepare('SELECT id, accountId FROM campaigns WHERE id = ?').get(campaignId);
      if (!campaign) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'campaign not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, campaign.accountId)) return;
      return sendJson(res, 200, { collections: getCreativeCollectionsForCampaign(campaignId) });
    }

    // PATCH /api/creative-collections/:id — rename a collection.
    if (req.method === 'PATCH' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-collections'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_collections WHERE id = ?').get(id);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'collection not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const name = body.name !== undefined ? body.name : existing.name;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_collections SET name = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(name, now, id);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { id, name, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // DELETE /api/creative-collections/:id — deletes the collection and all
    // its requirements (a collection with no requirements left is just
    // clutter, and per-requirement deletes below already give a lighter-
    // weight undo path for a single mistaken add).
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-collections'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_collections WHERE id = ?').get(id);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'collection not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM creative_requirements WHERE collectionId = ?').run(id);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM creative_collections WHERE id = ?').run(id);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-collections/:id/requirements — add a requirement
    // (Channel + Media Type + Specs) to a collection. copyStructure is the
    // best-practice shape the client generated (Channel + Loop Stage +
    // Audience + Media Type) — stored as a snapshot, same reasoning as
    // creative_jobs.specsJson: the guidance a requirement was actually
    // drafted against shouldn't silently change if the underlying guidance
    // rules are revised later.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-collections' && parts[3] === 'requirements'){
      const collectionId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const collection = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM creative_collections WHERE id = ?').get(collectionId);
      if (!collection) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'collection not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, collection.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.channel) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'channel is required' });
      const id = generateId('REQ');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO creative_requirements
        (id, collectionId, channel, mediaType, specs, copyStructureJson, copyContentJson, status, createdAt, updatedAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(id, collectionId, body.channel, body.mediaType || null, body.specs || null,
          body.copyStructure ? JSON.stringify(body.copyStructure) : null,
          body.copyContent ? JSON.stringify(body.copyContent) : '{}',
          'Draft', now, now);
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_collections SET updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(now, collectionId);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, collectionId, channel: body.channel, mediaType: body.mediaType || null, specs: body.specs || null, status: 'Draft', createdAt: now, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // PATCH /api/creative-requirements/:id — update copy content/status on
    // a requirement (Ready for Handoff / Approved requires copyContent to
    // have at least one non-empty field — an empty draft can't be handed
    // off, same "check the resulting value" gating pattern as creative_jobs).
    if (req.method === 'PATCH' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-requirements'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const current = db.prepare(
        `SELECT creative_requirements.*, creative_collections.accountId AS accountId FROM creative_requirements
         JOIN creative_collections ON creative_collections.id = creative_requirements.collectionId
         WHERE creative_requirements.id = ?`
      ).get(id);
      if (!current) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'requirement not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, current.accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const copyContent = body.copyContent !== undefined ? body.copyContent : (current.copyContentJson ? JSON.parse(current.copyContentJson) : {});
      const status = body.status !== undefined ? body.status : current.status;
      if (body.status !== undefined && !CREATIVE_REQUIREMENT_STATUSES.includes(status)){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `status must be one of: ${CREATIVE_REQUIREMENT_STATUSES.join(', ')}` });
      }
      const hasContent = copyContent && Object.values(copyContent).some(v => (v || '').toString().trim().length);
      if (status !== 'Draft' && !hasContent){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'Add copy content before moving this requirement past Draft.' });
      }
      const specs = body.specs !== undefined ? body.specs : current.specs;
      const mediaType = body.mediaType !== undefined ? body.mediaType : current.mediaType;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_requirements SET mediaType = ?, specs = ?, copyContentJson = ?, status = ?, updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(mediaType, specs, JSON.stringify(copyContent), status, now, id);
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_collections SET updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(now, current.collectionId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { id, status, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // POST /api/creative-requirements/:id/clone — duplicate a requirement
    // within the same collection (closes the "no reuse mechanism" audit
    // finding, per Section 4e of the scoping doc) — e.g. the same Display
    // ad copy structure reused for a second size. Clone always resets to
    // Draft — a cloned requirement is new work, not automatically approved
    // because its source was.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-requirements' && parts[3] === 'clone'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const source = db.prepare(
        `SELECT creative_requirements.*, creative_collections.accountId AS accountId FROM creative_requirements
         JOIN creative_collections ON creative_collections.id = creative_requirements.collectionId
         WHERE creative_requirements.id = ?`
      ).get(id);
      if (!source) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'requirement not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, source.accountId)) return;
      const newId = generateId('REQ');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO creative_requirements
        (id, collectionId, channel, mediaType, specs, copyStructureJson, copyContentJson, status, createdAt, updatedAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(newId, source.collectionId, source.channel, source.mediaType, source.specs, source.copyStructureJson, source.copyContentJson, 'Draft', now, now);
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_collections SET updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(now, source.collectionId);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id: newId, collectionId: source.collectionId, channel: source.channel, mediaType: source.mediaType, specs: source.specs, status: 'Draft', createdAt: now, updatedAt: now });
    }

    // DELETE /api/creative-requirements/:id
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'creative-requirements'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare(
        `SELECT creative_requirements.*, creative_collections.accountId AS accountId FROM creative_requirements
         JOIN creative_collections ON creative_collections.id = creative_requirements.collectionId
         WHERE creative_requirements.id = ?`
      ).get(id);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'requirement not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM creative_requirements WHERE id = ?').run(id);
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('UPDATE creative_collections SET updatedAt = ? WHERE id = ?').run(now, existing.collectionId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true });
    }


    // ---------- MMM data inputs (Deliverable #8) ----------

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-categories — the fixed 18-category
    // taxonomy, plus whichever of them this account has actually included.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-categories'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      return sendJson(res, 200, { allCategories: MMM_CATEGORIES, included });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/mmm-categories — save which categories this
    // client has selected as applicable. Editable any time (not locked after
    // first save, per direct instruction) — a whole-array save, same
    // convention as /utm-config.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-categories'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const included = Array.isArray(body.included) ? body.included : [];
      const invalid = included.filter(c => !MMM_CATEGORIES.includes(c));
      if (invalid.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `Unknown categor${invalid.length > 1 ? 'ies' : 'y'}: ${invalid.join(', ')}` });
      db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET mmmIncludedCategoriesJson = ? WHERE accountId = ?').run(JSON.stringify(included), accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { included });
    }

    // GET /api/onboarding-survey/modules — static, not account-scoped: the
    // question set itself. Frontend calls this once to render the form.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 2 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'onboarding-survey-modules'){
      return sendJson(res, 200, { coreQuestions: ONBOARDING_CORE_QUESTIONS, industryModules: ONBOARDING_INDUSTRY_MODULES });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/onboarding-survey — this account's saved
    // answers, both layers.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'onboarding-survey'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      return sendJson(res, 200, getOnboardingSurvey(accountId));
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/onboarding-survey — merge-save both layers.
    // Editable any time. industryCode must be one of the fixed module codes
    // (or null/empty to clear it); industryAnswers is only meaningful once
    // an industryCode is set, but is stored as given either way — clearing
    // the code doesn't wipe previously-entered answers, in case the account
    // switches back.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'onboarding-survey'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const existing = getOnboardingSurvey(accountId);
      const core = body.core && typeof body.core === 'object' ? Object.assign({}, existing.core, body.core) : existing.core;
      const validKeys = new Set(ONBOARDING_CORE_QUESTIONS.map(q => q.key));
      Object.keys(core).forEach(k => { if (!validKeys.has(k)) delete core[k]; });
      let industryCode = existing.industryCode;
      if (body.industryCode !== undefined){
        if (body.industryCode === null || body.industryCode === ''){ industryCode = null; }
        else if (ONBOARDING_INDUSTRY_MODULES.some(m => m.code === body.industryCode)){ industryCode = body.industryCode; }
        else return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `industryCode must be one of: ${ONBOARDING_INDUSTRY_MODULES.map(m => m.code).join(', ')}` });
      }
      const industryAnswers = body.industryAnswers && typeof body.industryAnswers === 'object' ? Object.assign({}, existing.industryAnswers, body.industryAnswers) : existing.industryAnswers;
      db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET onboardingSurveyJson = ?, onboardingIndustryCode = ?, onboardingIndustryAnswersJson = ? WHERE accountId = ?')
        .run(JSON.stringify(core), industryCode, JSON.stringify(industryAnswers), accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { core, industryCode, industryAnswers });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/stage-weights — buildout item #7. Returns the
    // default (global) weights, a suggestion computed from this account's
    // onboarding-survey answers (null if the relevant questions aren't
    // answered yet), and whatever override the account has actually saved
    // (null until they explicitly apply/save one — a suggestion is never
    // silently treated as the active override).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'stage-weights'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson, minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const survey = getOnboardingSurvey(accountId);
      const hasSignal = survey.core.avgTimeToTransaction != null || survey.core.repeatVsOneTime
        || survey.core.loyalTransactionCount != null || survey.core.repeatIntervalShrinksWithTenure;
      let override = null;
      try { override = account.stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson ? JSON.parse(account.stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson) : null; } catch (e) { override = null; }
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        default: DEFAULT_STAGE_BUDGET_WEIGHTS,
        suggested: hasSignal ? suggestStageBudgetWeights(survey.core) : null,
        override,
        minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled: !!account.minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled,
        suggestedMinViableSpendMultiplier: suggestMinViableSpendMultiplier(survey.core),
        minViableSpendReferenceAov: MIN_VIABLE_SPEND_REFERENCE_AOV
      });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/stage-weights — save (or clear, with
    // weights:null) the account's stage-budget-weight override, and/or
    // toggle AOV-driven minimum-viable-spend calibration. Weights, if
    // provided, must cover exactly the 5 Media Loop stages and sum to
    // ~1.00 (±0.01, floating-point slack) — a bad split silently applied
    // would misallocate every future recommended mix on this account.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'stage-weights'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const updates = [];
      const params = [];
      if (body.weights !== undefined){
        if (body.weights === null){
          updates.push('stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson = ?'); params.push(null);
        } else {
          const stages = ['Awareness', 'Consideration', 'Purchase', 'Loyalty', 'Advocacy'];
          const missing = stages.filter(s => body.weights[s] == null || isNaN(Number(body.weights[s])));
          if (missing.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `weights must include a number for every stage: ${missing.join(', ')}` });
          const sum = stages.reduce((s, k) => s + Number(body.weights[k]), 0);
          if (Math.abs(sum - 1) > 0.01) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `weights must sum to 1.00 — got ${sum.toFixed(3)}` });
          const clean = Object.fromEntries(stages.map(s => [s, Number(body.weights[s])]));
          updates.push('stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson = ?'); params.push(JSON.stringify(clean));
        }
      }
      if (body.minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled !== undefined){
        updates.push('minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled = ?'); params.push(body.minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled ? 1 : 0);
      }
      if (!updates.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'nothing to update — pass weights and/or minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled' });
      params.push(accountId);
      db.prepare(`UPDATE accounts SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE accountId = ?`).run(...params);
      const refreshed = db.prepare('SELECT stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson, minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      let override = null;
      try { override = refreshed.stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson ? JSON.parse(refreshed.stageBudgetWeightsOverrideJson) : null; } catch (e) { override = null; }
      return sendJson(res, 200, { override, minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled: !!refreshed.minViableSpendCalibrationEnabled });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/measurement-coverage — this account's saved
    // answer to "what can you actually measure today" (buildout item #1).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'measurement-coverage'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId, measurementCoverageJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { coverage: getMeasurementCoverage(accountId), allCategories: MMM_CATEGORIES, hasSaved: !!account.measurementCoverageJson });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/measurement-coverage — save/update. Editable
    // any time, same convention as mmm-categories. Whole-object save.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'measurement-coverage'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const incoming = body.coverage && typeof body.coverage === 'object' ? body.coverage : {};
      const merged = Object.assign(defaultMeasurementCoverage(), incoming);
      ['callTracking', 'qrTracking', 'utmDiscipline', 'firstPartyMatchBack'].forEach(k => {
        const block = merged[k] && typeof merged[k] === 'object' ? merged[k] : {};
        const channels = Array.isArray(block.channels) ? block.channels.filter(c => MMM_CATEGORIES.includes(c)) : [];
        merged[k] = { enabled: !!block.enabled, channels };
      });
      merged.mmm = !!merged.mmm;
      merged.dmaMatchMarket = !!merged.dmaMatchMarket;
      merged.neuroscience = !!merged.neuroscience;
      db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET measurementCoverageJson = ? WHERE accountId = ?').run(JSON.stringify(merged), accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { coverage: merged });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/measurement-confidence — buildout item #2: the
    // per-channel confidence label, derived from measurement-coverage plus
    // the fixed capability table. Restricted to this account's included MMM
    // categories when that inclusion list has been saved (same pattern as
    // mmm-categories), otherwise every category.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'measurement-confidence'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId, mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
