      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      const categories = included.length ? included : MMM_CATEGORIES;
      const coverage = getMeasurementCoverage(accountId);
      const confidence = categories.map(c => computeMeasurementConfidence(c, coverage));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { confidence });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/partner-capability-requests — buildout item #5.
    // Every request on file for this account, most recent first, plus the
    // illustrative-partner info block per capability and this account's
    // current measurement-coverage flag for each (so the client, seeing
    // "requested," can also see once it's actually been turned on via
    // Measurement Coverage).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'partner-capability-requests'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const requests = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM partner_capability_requests WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all(accountId);
      const coverage = getMeasurementCoverage(accountId);
      const capabilities = VALID_PARTNER_CAPABILITIES.map(key => ({
        key, ...PARTNER_CAPABILITY_INFO[key],
        enabledOnAccount: !!coverage[key],
        requests: requests.filter(r => r.capability === key)
      }));
      return sendJson(res, 200, { capabilities });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/partner-capability-requests — submit a new
    // request. Never auto-turns on the capability itself (that stays a
    // Measurement Coverage edit, a deliberate real-world confirmation step,
    // not something a request form should silently assume) — this only
    // logs that the account asked, so it has a real next step to point to.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'partner-capability-requests'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT accountId FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!VALID_PARTNER_CAPABILITIES.includes(body.capability)){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `capability must be one of: ${VALID_PARTNER_CAPABILITIES.join(', ')}` });
      }
      const id = generateId('PCR');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO partner_capability_requests (id, accountId, capability, note, status, createdAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)')
        .run(id, accountId, body.capability, (body.note || '').trim() || null, 'requested', now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, accountId, capability: body.capability, note: body.note || null, status: 'requested', createdAt: now });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-category-mapping — the partner-facing bucket
    // label for each included category (defaults to the category's own name
    // until remapped).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-category-mapping'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      return sendJson(res, 200, { mappings: getMmmCategoryMapping(accountId, included) });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/mmm-category-mapping — set the partner bucket
    // label for one or more included categories. Consolidation happens
    // simply by giving two+ categories the same partnerBucketLabel — the
    // partner export below sums whatever shares a label.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-category-mapping'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const mappings = Array.isArray(body.mappings) ? body.mappings : [];
      const invalid = mappings.filter(m => !included.includes(m.rawCategory));
      if (invalid.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `Can only map included categories: ${invalid.map(m => m.rawCategory).join(', ')}` });
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const upsert = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO mmm_category_mappings (accountId, rawCategory, partnerBucketLabel, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?)
        ON CONFLICT(accountId, rawCategory) DO UPDATE SET partnerBucketLabel = excluded.partnerBucketLabel, updatedAt = excluded.updatedAt`);
      mappings.forEach(m => {
        const label = (m.partnerBucketLabel || '').trim() || m.rawCategory;
        upsert.run(accountId, m.rawCategory, label, now);
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { mappings: getMmmCategoryMapping(accountId, included) });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/mmm-inputs — log one period+category's real
    // delivery + spend/outcome totals. Reach, Impressions, and Spend are all
    // required (per direct instruction: "require monthly reach/impressions
    // per selected category", extended round 86 to also require Spend per
    // category) — only outcomeMetric/outcomeValue remain optional, since not
    // every category has a trackable outcome metric. The category must be
    // one of the fixed 18, and — once this account has saved an inclusion
    // list — must actually be included.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-inputs'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.periodLabel || !body.channel) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'periodLabel and channel are required' });
      if (!MMM_CATEGORIES.includes(body.channel)) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `Unknown category: ${body.channel}` });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      if (included.length && !included.includes(body.channel)){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `"${body.channel}" is not one of this account's included categories.` });
      }
      if (body.reach == null || body.reach === '' || body.impressions == null || body.impressions === '' || body.spend == null || body.spend === ''){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'spend, reach, and impressions are required for every logged row.' });
      }
      const id = generateId('MMI');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO mmm_inputs (id, accountId, periodLabel, channel, spend, outcomeMetric, outcomeValue, reach, impressions, createdAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(id, accountId, body.periodLabel, body.channel,
          Number(body.spend),
          body.outcomeMetric || null,
          body.outcomeValue != null && body.outcomeValue !== '' ? Number(body.outcomeValue) : null,
          Number(body.reach), Number(body.impressions), now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, {
        id, periodLabel: body.periodLabel, channel: body.channel, spend: body.spend,
        outcomeMetric: body.outcomeMetric, outcomeValue: body.outcomeValue,
        reach: body.reach, impressions: body.impressions, createdAt: now
      });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-inputs — every manually-logged period+
    // category row (most recent first, individually editable/deletable, same
    // as before), PLUS (round 89) the campaign-sourced roll-up (every
    // campaign's line items summed by month via getCampaignMmmRollup — see
    // that function's comment), PLUS a real completeness matrix against this
    // account's included categories computed from BOTH sources COMBINED
    // (getEffectiveMmmTotals): for every period either source has data for,
    // which included categories actually have Spend+Reach+Impressions on
    // file (from manual entry, campaign actuals, or both) and which are
    // still missing. Still never a modeled output — just what's on file.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-inputs'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      const { totals, manualRows, campaignRollup } = getEffectiveMmmTotals(accountId);
      const periodList = [...new Set(totals.map(t => t.periodLabel))].sort().reverse();
      const totalsByKey = Object.fromEntries(totals.map(t => [`${t.periodLabel}||${t.category}`, t]));
      const matrix = periodList.map(periodLabel => ({
        periodLabel,
        categories: included.map(cat => {
          const t = totalsByKey[`${periodLabel}||${cat}`];
          return { category: cat, complete: !!(t && t.spend != null && t.reach != null && t.impressions != null) };
        })
      }));
      const totalRequired = periodList.length * included.length;
      const totalComplete = matrix.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.categories.filter(c => c.complete).length, 0);
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        inputs: manualRows, campaignRollup,
        periodCount: periodList.length, channelCount: new Set(totals.map(t => t.category)).size,
        included, matrix, totalRequired, totalComplete
      });
    }

    // DELETE /api/mmm-inputs/:id
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'mmm-inputs'){
      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM mmm_inputs WHERE id = ?').get(id);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'mmm input not found' });
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, existing.accountId)) return;
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM mmm_inputs WHERE id = ?').run(id);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-partner-export — every period+category total
    // (round 89: manual entry AND the campaign-sourced roll-up, combined via
    // POST /api/accounts/:id/uploads — the shared, scanned upload entry
    // point (round 132bl). Body: { purpose, filename, mimeType, dataUrl
    // (base64 data URI), uploadedBy }. Runs PII screening on any text-like
    // MIME type, then the virus-scan hook (scanFileForThreats, defined
    // above with the rest of the upload-security helpers), on EVERY upload
    // regardless of purpose — rejects and records the attempt (never
    // silently drops it) on either failure, and only persists dataUrl for
    // an upload that actually passes both.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.dataUrl || typeof body.dataUrl !== 'string') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'dataUrl (base64 data URI) is required' });
      const match = body.dataUrl.match(/^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/);
      if (!match) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'dataUrl must be a base64 data URI, e.g. "data:text/csv;base64,..."' });
      const mimeType = match[1];
      const buffer = Buffer.from(match[2], 'base64');
      const sizeBytes = buffer.length;
      const sha256 = crypto_uploads.createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex');
      const id = generateId('UPL');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const uploadedBy = (body.uploadedBy || '').trim() || null;
      const purpose = (body.purpose || 'general').trim();

      const isTextLike = /^text\/|csv|json$/i.test(mimeType);
      const piiFindings = isTextLike ? scanTextForPii(buffer.toString('utf8')) : [];
      if (piiFindings.length){
        db.prepare(`INSERT INTO uploaded_files (id, accountId, purpose, originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, dataUrl, status, piiScanFindings, virusScanStatus, virusScanProvider, virusScanDetail, uploadedBy, createdAt)
          VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,NULL,?,?,NULL,NULL,NULL,?,?)`)
          .run(id, accountId, purpose, body.filename || null, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, 'rejected_pii', JSON.stringify(piiFindings), uploadedBy, now);
        return sendJson(res, 422, { id, status: 'rejected_pii', piiFindings });
      }

      const devBypass = process.env.ALLOW_UNSCANNED_UPLOADS_DEV_ONLY === 'true';
      const scan = await scanFileForThreats(buffer);
      let effectiveScan = scan;
      if (scan.status === 'not_configured' && devBypass){
        effectiveScan = { status: 'not_configured_dev_bypass', provider: null, detail: 'ALLOW_UNSCANNED_UPLOADS_DEV_ONLY is set — accepted WITHOUT a real virus scan. Never set this in production.' };
      }
      const accepted = effectiveScan.status === 'clean' || effectiveScan.status === 'not_configured_dev_bypass';
      if (!accepted){
        const status = effectiveScan.status === 'infected' ? 'rejected_virus' : 'rejected_scan_unavailable';
        db.prepare(`INSERT INTO uploaded_files (id, accountId, purpose, originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, dataUrl, status, piiScanFindings, virusScanStatus, virusScanProvider, virusScanDetail, uploadedBy, createdAt)
          VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,NULL,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
          .run(id, accountId, purpose, body.filename || null, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, status, JSON.stringify([]), effectiveScan.status, effectiveScan.provider, effectiveScan.detail, uploadedBy, now);
        return sendJson(res, 422, { id, status, virusScan: effectiveScan });
      }

      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO uploaded_files (id, accountId, purpose, originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, dataUrl, status, piiScanFindings, virusScanStatus, virusScanProvider, virusScanDetail, uploadedBy, createdAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(id, accountId, purpose, body.filename || null, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, body.dataUrl, 'accepted', JSON.stringify([]), effectiveScan.status, effectiveScan.provider, effectiveScan.detail, uploadedBy, now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, status: 'accepted', virusScan: effectiveScan, sizeBytes, sha256 });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/uploads?purpose=... — real audit trail of every
    // upload attempt (accepted AND rejected) for this account, never just
    // the successful ones — a rejected upload is itself a real event worth
    // being able to see. dataUrl is omitted from the list response (only
    // returned on the POST that created it) to keep this endpoint light.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost');
      const purpose = url.searchParams.get('purpose');
      const rows = purpose
        ? db.prepare('SELECT id, purpose, originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, status, piiScanFindings, virusScanStatus, virusScanProvider, virusScanDetail, uploadedBy, createdAt FROM uploaded_files WHERE accountId = ? AND purpose = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all(accountId, purpose)
        : db.prepare('SELECT id, purpose, originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes, sha256, status, piiScanFindings, virusScanStatus, virusScanProvider, virusScanDetail, uploadedBy, createdAt FROM uploaded_files WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all(accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploads: rows.map(r => ({ ...r, piiScanFindings: r.piiScanFindings ? JSON.parse(r.piiScanFindings) : [] })) });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/brand-writing-samples — commit a Brand sample
    // writing/presentation. Two source shapes, per the design note above:
    //  - sourceType 'file': body.uploadedFileId must reference an
    //    uploaded_files row for this account with status 'accepted' and
    //    purpose 'brand_writing_sample' (i.e. it already passed the scan
    //    pipeline via POST .../uploads before this call).
    //  - sourceType 'link': body.sourceUrl is required instead (Google
    //    Docs/Slides, or any other link-only reference) — no file ever
    //    reaches this server, so no scan applies.
    // category must be one of BRAND_WRITING_SAMPLE_CATEGORIES — rejected as
    // an unknown category otherwise, same enforcement pattern as the
    // Verilume budget category mapping. docDate and title are required in
    // both shapes: "always categorizing them by purpose and date" was a
    // direct instruction, not an optional field.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'brand-writing-samples'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const category = (body.category || '').trim();
      const validCategory = BRAND_WRITING_SAMPLE_CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === category);
      if (!validCategory) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `category must be one of: ${BRAND_WRITING_SAMPLE_CATEGORIES.map(c => c.id).join(', ')}` });
      if (!body.title || !body.title.trim()) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'title is required' });
      if (!body.docDate || !body.docDate.trim()) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'docDate is required — this feature always categorizes by purpose AND date' });
      const sourceType = body.sourceType === 'link' ? 'link' : 'file';

      let uploadedFileId = null;
      if (sourceType === 'file'){
        if (!body.uploadedFileId) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'uploadedFileId is required for sourceType "file" — upload via POST /api/accounts/:id/uploads (purpose: brand_writing_sample) first' });
        const uploadedFile = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM uploaded_files WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(body.uploadedFileId, accountId);
        if (!uploadedFile) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'uploadedFileId does not reference an upload for this account' });
        if (uploadedFile.purpose !== 'brand_writing_sample') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `uploadedFileId's upload has purpose "${uploadedFile.purpose}", expected "brand_writing_sample"` });
        if (uploadedFile.status !== 'accepted') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `this upload has status "${uploadedFile.status}" — only an accepted (scanned, clean) upload can be attached as a brand writing sample` });
        uploadedFileId = uploadedFile.id;
      } else {
        if (!body.sourceUrl || !body.sourceUrl.trim()) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'sourceUrl is required for sourceType "link" (e.g. a Google Docs or Google Slides share link)' });
      }

      const id = generateId('BWS');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO brand_writing_samples (id, accountId, uploadedFileId, sourceType, sourceUrl, title, category, docDate, notes, uploadedBy, createdAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(id, accountId, uploadedFileId, sourceType, sourceType === 'link' ? body.sourceUrl.trim() : null, body.title.trim(), category, body.docDate.trim(), (body.notes || '').trim() || null, (body.uploadedBy || '').trim() || null, now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, status: 'saved' });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/brand-writing-samples?category=... — list,
    // newest-by-docDate first, joined with the underlying uploaded_files
    // row (filename/mimeType/sizeBytes) when this sample is file-backed.
    // Always returns the fixed category list too, so the frontend never
    // has to hardcode it separately from what the server will accept.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'brand-writing-samples'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost');
      const category = url.searchParams.get('category');
      const rows = category
        ? db.prepare('SELECT * FROM brand_writing_samples WHERE accountId = ? AND category = ? ORDER BY docDate DESC, createdAt DESC').all(accountId, category)
        : db.prepare('SELECT * FROM brand_writing_samples WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY docDate DESC, createdAt DESC').all(accountId);
      const samples = rows.map(r => {
        const uploadedFile = r.uploadedFileId ? db.prepare('SELECT originalFilename, mimeType, sizeBytes FROM uploaded_files WHERE id = ?').get(r.uploadedFileId) : null;
        return { ...r, file: uploadedFile || null };
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { samples, categories: BRAND_WRITING_SAMPLE_CATEGORIES });
    }

    // DELETE /api/accounts/:id/brand-writing-samples/:sampleId
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'brand-writing-samples'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const sampleId = decodeURIComponent(parts[4]);
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM brand_writing_samples WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(sampleId, accountId);
      if (!existing) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'brand writing sample not found' });
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM brand_writing_samples WHERE id = ?').run(sampleId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true });
    }

    // POST /api/market-population-master — CX Ops bulk-loads real
    // population figures (e.g. from a Census Bureau ZCTA export or an
    // account-supplied file) — { rows: [{zip, population}], sourceLabel }.
    // Not account-scoped: population is a real-world fact independent of
    // any one client. Upserts — re-loading a zip replaces its prior figure
    // rather than duplicating it, so this can be safely re-run as fresher
    // Census data becomes available.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 2 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'market-population-master'){
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!Array.isArray(body.rows) || !body.rows.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'rows (array of {zip, population}) is required' });
      if (!body.sourceLabel || !body.sourceLabel.trim()) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'sourceLabel is required — where did this population data come from?' });
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      let inserted = 0, errors = [];
      body.rows.forEach((r, i) => {
        if (!r.zip || !/^\d{5}$/.test(String(r.zip))) { errors.push(`row ${i}: zip must be a 5-digit code, got "${r.zip}"`); return; }
        if (r.population == null || isNaN(Number(r.population)) || Number(r.population) < 0) { errors.push(`row ${i}: population must be a non-negative number, got "${r.population}"`); return; }
        db.prepare(`INSERT INTO zip_population_master (zip, population, sourceLabel, updatedAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?)
          ON CONFLICT(zip) DO UPDATE SET population = excluded.population, sourceLabel = excluded.sourceLabel, updatedAt = excluded.updatedAt`)
          .run(String(r.zip), Number(r.population), body.sourceLabel.trim(), now);
        inserted++;
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { inserted, errors, totalZipsOnFile: db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM zip_population_master').get().n });
    }

    // GET /api/market-population-master/status — honest state of the
    // master file (never implied to be complete — just how many zips are on
    // file and from what source), so the Market Optimization UI can show
    // "no population data loaded yet" rather than silently computing
    // against nothing.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 3 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'market-population-master' && parts[2] === 'status'){
      const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM zip_population_master').get().n;
      const sources = db.prepare('SELECT DISTINCT sourceLabel FROM zip_population_master').all().map(r => r.sourceLabel);
      const demoAttrs = db.prepare('SELECT DISTINCT attribute FROM zip_demographic_master').all().map(r => r.attribute);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { zipsLoaded: count, sources, demographicAttributes: demoAttrs });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/market-customer-uploads — commits a
    // market-optimization customer file that has ALREADY passed the secure
    // upload pipeline above (must reference a real uploaded_files row with
    // status 'accepted' and purpose 'market_optimization' — never accepts
    // raw rows without that check, so this can't be used to route around
    // PII/virus screening). Body: { uploadedFileId, label, weightMode,
    // audit, rows: [{zip, customerCount, revenue}] } — rows are already
    // zip-level aggregates by the time they get here (the client aggregates
    // raw transaction-level rows itself, same "parse then confirm" pattern
    // as everywhere else in this build); `audit` is the real row-by-row
    // validation summary the client computed (see mouBuildMappedRows /
    // mouAggregateByZip in portal.html) — stored as-is, not recomputed,
    // since the exclusions already happened before rows got aggregated.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'market-customer-uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.uploadedFileId) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'uploadedFileId is required — this file must go through POST .../uploads first' });
      const uploadedFile = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM uploaded_files WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(body.uploadedFileId, accountId);
      if (!uploadedFile) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such uploaded file on this account' });
      if (uploadedFile.status !== 'accepted') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `that file's upload status is "${uploadedFile.status}", not "accepted" — it must pass the PII and virus scan first` });
      if (uploadedFile.purpose !== 'market_optimization') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `that file was uploaded for purpose "${uploadedFile.purpose}", not "market_optimization"` });
      if (!Array.isArray(body.rows) || !body.rows.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'rows (array of {zip, customerCount, revenue?}) is required' });
      const weightMode = body.weightMode === 'revenue' ? 'revenue' : 'count';
      const rowErrors = [];
      body.rows.forEach((r, i) => {
        if (!r.zip || !/^\d{5}$/.test(String(r.zip))) rowErrors.push(`row ${i}: zip must be a 5-digit US zip, got "${r.zip}"`);
        if (r.customerCount == null || isNaN(Number(r.customerCount)) || Number(r.customerCount) < 0) rowErrors.push(`row ${i}: customerCount must be a non-negative number, got "${r.customerCount}"`);
        if (r.revenue != null && (isNaN(Number(r.revenue)) || Number(r.revenue) < 0)) rowErrors.push(`row ${i}: revenue must be a non-negative number if provided, got "${r.revenue}"`);
      });
      if (weightMode === 'revenue' && body.rows.some(r => r.revenue == null)){
        return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'weightMode is "revenue" but not every zip has a revenue total — either provide revenue for every zip or use weightMode "count".' });
      }
      if (rowErrors.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'row validation failed', rowErrors });
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const id = generateId('MKTUP');
      const auditJson = body.audit ? JSON.stringify(body.audit) : null;
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO market_customer_uploads (id, accountId, uploadedFileId, label, createdAt, weightMode, auditJson) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)')
        .run(id, accountId, body.uploadedFileId, body.label || null, now, weightMode, auditJson);
      const insertRow = db.prepare('INSERT INTO market_customer_rows (id, marketUploadId, zip, customerCount, revenue) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
      body.rows.forEach(r => insertRow.run(generateId('MKTROW'), id, String(r.zip), Number(r.customerCount), r.revenue != null ? Number(r.revenue) : null));
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, rowCount: body.rows.length, weightMode });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/market-customer-uploads/:uploadId/analysis —
    // the real match-market readout for one committed customer upload:
    // penetration index, composite score/opportunity tier, a Holdout
    // carve-out, demographic index (if any demographic data is on file),
    // the index-based matched test/control pairs (Holdout excluded), and
    // the audit trail captured at commit time. Every "no data" state is
    // reported explicitly rather than silently omitted. Accepts optional
    // ?volumeWeight=&indexWeight=&holdoutFraction= query params so the
    // composite-score weighting and Holdout size — both explicitly flagged
    // as open assumptions in the design doc, not settled facts — can be
    // adjusted per account without a code change; defaults to 2/3, 1/3, 1/6.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'market-customer-uploads' && parts[5] === 'analysis'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM market_customer_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such market customer upload on this account' });
      const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost');
      const volumeWeight = url.searchParams.has('volumeWeight') ? Number(url.searchParams.get('volumeWeight')) : (2 / 3);
      const indexWeight = url.searchParams.has('indexWeight') ? Number(url.searchParams.get('indexWeight')) : (1 / 3);
      const holdoutFraction = url.searchParams.has('holdoutFraction') ? Number(url.searchParams.get('holdoutFraction')) : (1 / 6);
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT zip, customerCount, revenue FROM market_customer_rows WHERE marketUploadId = ?').all(uploadId);
      const penetration = computePenetrationIndex(rows, upload.weightMode);
      const composite = computeCompositeScore(penetration, { volumeWeight, indexWeight });
      const holdout = computeHoldoutSplit(composite.rows, holdoutFraction);
      const demographic = computeDemographicIndex(rows.map(r => r.zip));
      const matching = computeMatchedMarketPairs(composite.rows, demographic, holdout.holdoutZips);
      const audit = upload.auditJson ? JSON.parse(upload.auditJson) : null;
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        uploadId, label: upload.label, rowCount: rows.length, weightMode: upload.weightMode,
        penetration: composite.rows, compositeWeights: composite.weights,
        holdout: { zips: holdout.holdoutZips, fraction: holdoutFraction, note: holdout.note },
        demographic, matching, audit
      });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads — Marketing Budget
    // Upload, format-flexibility test (separate feature from MMM's cfi*
    // bulk import above — per direct instruction, not the same thing).
    // Body: { year, uploadedFileId, fileName, grid } where grid is the FULL
    // raw sheet (array of arrays of cell text, from client-side SheetJS —
    // header row NOT assumed, since real client files vary). uploadedFileId
    // must reference a real uploaded_files row (purpose 'marketing_budget')
    // that already passed the shared PII/virus-scan pipeline — same
    // must-scan-first rule as market-customer-uploads. Runs
    // analyzeMarketingBudgetGrid and stores the raw grid + analysis for the
    // confirm step below; does not commit any line items yet.
    // Round 132bx follow-on (2026-08-16), per direct instruction: "Uploads
    // should be single year only with user selecting year before uploading
    // file. A budget year can't be uploaded again." year is now required
    // (the user picks it BEFORE choosing a file, on the front end), and a
    // second upload for a year that already has a CONFIRMED budget is
    // rejected outright — the front end should be pointing the user at the
    // existing upload's own review screen (to adjust category totals in
    // place) or its delete action instead of letting a duplicate through.
    // A year with only a needs_mapping/pending_category_mapping upload
    // (never actually confirmed) is NOT blocked — that's an abandoned or
    // still-in-progress attempt, not a real budget yet, so starting over
    // with a fresh file for that year is fine.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const body = await readBody(req);
      const year = parseInt(body.year, 10);
      if (!year || year < 2000 || year > 2100) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'a valid year is required — select the budget year before uploading a file' });
      if (!body.uploadedFileId) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'uploadedFileId is required — this file must go through POST .../uploads (purpose=marketing_budget) first' });
      const uploadedFile = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM uploaded_files WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(body.uploadedFileId, accountId);
      if (!uploadedFile || uploadedFile.status !== 'accepted') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'uploadedFileId must reference a scanned, accepted upload on this account' });
      if (!Array.isArray(body.grid) || !body.grid.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'grid (array of rows) is required' });
      const existingConfirmed = db.prepare("SELECT id, fileName, confirmedAt FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE accountId = ? AND year = ? AND status = 'confirmed'").get(accountId, year);
      if (existingConfirmed){
        return sendJson(res, 409, {
          error: `${year} already has a confirmed budget ("${existingConfirmed.fileName || 'untitled file'}", confirmed ${existingConfirmed.confirmedAt}). Adjust its category totals from that upload's own review screen instead of uploading again, or delete it first if you truly need to start over.`,
          existingUploadId: existingConfirmed.id,
          year
        });
      }
      const analysis = analyzeMarketingBudgetGrid(body.grid);
      const id = generateId('MBU');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      db.prepare(`INSERT INTO marketing_budget_uploads (id, accountId, uploadedFileId, fileName, gridJson, layout, analysisJson, status, year, createdAt)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
        .run(id, accountId, body.uploadedFileId, body.fileName || uploadedFile.originalFilename || null, JSON.stringify(body.grid), analysis.layout, JSON.stringify(analysis), analysis.status, year, now);
      return sendJson(res, 201, { id, year, layout: analysis.layout, status: analysis.status, note: analysis.note, headerRows: analysis.headerRows, blocks: analysis.blocks, monthsRecognized: analysis.monthsRecognized, likelyGrandTotalRowIdxs: analysis.likelyGrandTotalRowIdxs, targetFields: MARKETING_BUDGET_TARGET_FIELDS });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId/confirm —
    // commits real line items using a human-confirmed mapping. Body:
    // { blocks: [{ headerRowIdx, categoryColIdx, statusColIdx, monthCols:
    // {jan: colIdx, ...}, totalColIdx }] } — one entry per block (matching
    // the analysis' blocks[].headerRowIdx). Any block/column omitted is
    // simply not imported (never guessed). Re-derives data rows for each
    // block from the stored grid using each block's own headerRowIdx and
    // the next block's headerRowIdx as the boundary, same logic as analysis.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads' && parts[5] === 'confirm'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!Array.isArray(body.blocks) || !body.blocks.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'blocks (array of confirmed column mappings) is required' });
      const grid = JSON.parse(upload.gridJson).map(r => (r || []).map(c => (c == null ? '' : String(c))));
      const allHeaderRows = body.blocks.map(b => b.headerRowIdx).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ?').run(uploadId);
      let inserted = 0, skipped = 0;
      const now2 = new Date().toISOString();
      body.blocks.forEach(b => {
        const nextHeaderIdx = allHeaderRows.find(h => h > b.headerRowIdx);
        const boundary = nextHeaderIdx != null ? nextHeaderIdx : grid.length;
        const excludeRows = new Set(Array.isArray(b.excludeRowIdxs) ? b.excludeRowIdxs : []);
        for (let r = b.headerRowIdx + 1; r < boundary; r++){
          if (excludeRows.has(r)){ skipped++; continue; }
          const row = grid[r] || [];
          if (!row.some(c => (c || '').trim() !== '')) continue;
          const category = b.categoryColIdx != null ? (row[b.categoryColIdx] || '').trim() : '';
          const status = b.statusColIdx != null ? (row[b.statusColIdx] || '').trim() : null;
          const total = b.totalColIdx != null ? mbuNumberOrNull(row[b.totalColIdx]) : null;
          if (!category){ skipped++; continue; }
          // A row whose category text is plainly a total/subtotal label
          // ("Total Marketing", "Grand Total", ...) is never a real line
          // item -- skip it here unconditionally, on top of whatever the
          // numeric subtotal detector already flagged as pre-checked
          // excludeRowIdxs. This is what actually prevents a ledger-style
          // total row (blank month cells, single bottom-line annual figure
          // — no per-item annual total to numerically match against) from
          // ever reaching marketing_budget_line_items in the first place,
          // rather than relying only on the frontend hiding it from view
          // after the fact, which left it permanently unmappable instead.
          if (mbuIsTotalLikeCategoryLabel(category)){ skipped++; continue; }
          MARKETING_BUDGET_MONTHS.forEach(m => {
            const colIdx = b.monthCols ? b.monthCols[m] : null;
            if (colIdx == null) return;
            const amount = mbuNumberOrNull(row[colIdx]);
            if (amount == null) return;
            db.prepare(`INSERT INTO marketing_budget_line_items (id, uploadId, accountId, category, status, month, amount, annualTotal, sourceRowIndex) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
              .run(generateId('MBLI'), uploadId, accountId, category, status, m, amount, total, r);
            inserted++;
          });
          if (!b.monthCols || !Object.keys(b.monthCols).length){
            // No month columns confirmed for this block — still record the category/total as a single annual-only row so it isn't silently lost.
            db.prepare(`INSERT INTO marketing_budget_line_items (id, uploadId, accountId, category, status, month, amount, annualTotal, sourceRowIndex) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
              .run(generateId('MBLI'), uploadId, accountId, category, status, null, null, total, r);
            inserted++;
          }
        }
      });
      // Column mapping confirmed and line items are now stored with their
      // RAW category text — but per direct instruction, this isn't
      // "available to use" yet. It still needs each distinct raw category
      // mapped to Verilume's own standard taxonomy (VERILUME_BUDGET_
      // CATEGORIES above), confirmed by the client, before anything reads
      // from it elsewhere in the platform.
      db.prepare('UPDATE marketing_budget_uploads SET status = ?, confirmedMappingJson = ?, confirmedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run('pending_category_mapping', JSON.stringify(body.blocks), now2, uploadId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploadId, status: 'pending_category_mapping', lineItemsInserted: inserted, rowsSkipped: skipped });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId/category-mapping
    // — the visual mapping view per direct instruction: "row by row mapping
    // based on the total not month." One row per DISTINCT raw category
    // found in this upload's committed line items, with its real annual
    // total (summed across whatever months/annualTotal are on file for it —
    // never per-month, matching the instruction), a heuristic Verilume-
    // category suggestion (never auto-applied), and its current confirmed
    // mapping if this has been done before (so re-opening this screen shows
    // prior answers, not a blank form).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads' && parts[5] === 'category-mapping'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT category, status, amount, annualTotal, verilumeCategory FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ?').all(uploadId);
      const byCategory = {};
      rows.forEach(r => {
        if (!byCategory[r.category]) byCategory[r.category] = { category: r.category, status: r.status, total: 0, hasAnnualTotal: false, verilumeCategory: r.verilumeCategory || null };
        // Prefer the file's own stated annual total when present (real
        // number, not a re-derived sum); fall back to summing whatever
        // monthly amounts are on file for rows with no annual total.
        // Round 132bx follow-on (2026-08-16), found while verifying the
        // total-row fix above against a ledger-style file (monthly figures
        // on every line item, no per-row annual total): the old `total ===
        // 0` gate on the amount-summing branch meant only the FIRST month's
        // amount ever got added -- every category's shown total silently
        // collapsed to a single month's spend instead of the real annual
        // sum the moment annualTotal wasn't present. Tracked explicitly with
        // hasAnnualTotal instead, since checking `=== 0` can't tell "no
        // months summed yet" apart from "already summed, happens to be 0."
        if (r.annualTotal != null){ byCategory[r.category].total = r.annualTotal; byCategory[r.category].hasAnnualTotal = true; }
        else if (!byCategory[r.category].hasAnnualTotal && r.amount != null) byCategory[r.category].total += r.amount;
      });
      const gaps = Object.values(byCategory).map(c => ({
        category: c.category,
        status: c.status,
        total: c.total,
        currentVerilumeCategory: c.verilumeCategory,
        // Round 132bx follow-on — a remembered mapping from this account's
        // own past confirm-categories decisions (see mbuRecallCategoryMapping)
        // wins over the generic rule-based heuristic when both exist, since
        // it reflects a real human choice for this exact raw text rather
        // than a keyword guess.
        suggested: c.verilumeCategory ? null : (mbuRecallCategoryMapping(accountId, c.category) || mbuSuggestVerilumeCategory(c.category))
      })).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);
      // Client-created categories (see confirm-categories below) — any
      // verilumeCategory value already saved on this upload that ISN'T one
      // of the 20 standard ones. Reported separately so the client's own
      // single-screen review can list them as their own rows alongside the
      // standard 20, not lose them into "Other/Uncategorized."
      const customCategoriesInUse = [...new Set(Object.values(byCategory)
        .map(c => c.verilumeCategory)
        .filter(v => v && !VERILUME_BUDGET_CATEGORIES.includes(v)))];
      const overrideRows = db.prepare('SELECT verilumeCategory, overrideTotal FROM marketing_budget_category_overrides WHERE uploadId = ?').all(uploadId);
      const overrides = {};
      overrideRows.forEach(r => { overrides[r.verilumeCategory] = r.overrideTotal; });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploadId, uploadStatus: upload.status, verilumeCategories: VERILUME_BUDGET_CATEGORIES, customCategoriesInUse, overrides, categories: gaps, allMapped: gaps.every(g => g.currentVerilumeCategory) });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId/category-overrides
    // — per direct instruction, the single-screen review lets a client
    // accept or manually edit the total shown for a Verilume category
    // rather than only ever seeing the computed sum of matched line items.
    // Body: { overrides: { verilumeCategory: amount|null } } — a null (or
    // omitted) value clears any existing override for that category, going
    // back to the computed sum. Never touches the underlying line items.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads' && parts[5] === 'category-overrides'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.overrides || typeof body.overrides !== 'object') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'overrides ({verilumeCategory: amount|null}) is required' });
      const now3b = new Date().toISOString();
      Object.entries(body.overrides).forEach(([verilumeCategory, amount]) => {
        if (amount === null || amount === '' || amount === undefined){
          db.prepare('DELETE FROM marketing_budget_category_overrides WHERE uploadId = ? AND verilumeCategory = ?').run(uploadId, verilumeCategory);
        } else {
          db.prepare(`INSERT INTO marketing_budget_category_overrides (uploadId, verilumeCategory, overrideTotal, updatedAt) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(uploadId, verilumeCategory) DO UPDATE SET overrideTotal = excluded.overrideTotal, updatedAt = excluded.updatedAt`)
            .run(uploadId, verilumeCategory, Number(amount), now3b);
        }
      });
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploadId, saved: true });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId/confirm-categories
    // — the confirm-with-option-to-correct step. Body: { mapping: {
    // rawCategory: verilumeCategory } }. Every verilumeCategory must either
    // be a real value from VERILUME_BUDGET_CATEGORIES, or a client-created
    // category — a "Client: <name>" -prefixed string this account defined
    // for itself instead of forcing a fit into the standard 20 (rejects
    // anything else as an unknown value rather than storing a typo
    // silently). Only marks the upload 'confirmed' — genuinely "available
    // to use" — once EVERY distinct raw category on this upload has a
    // mapping; otherwise it stays 'pending_category_mapping' and reports
    // exactly what's still missing, never a false "done."
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 6 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads' && parts[5] === 'confirm-categories'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const body = await readBody(req);
      if (!body.mapping || typeof body.mapping !== 'object') return sendJson(res, 400, { error: 'mapping ({rawCategory: verilumeCategory}) is required' });
      const invalid = Object.entries(body.mapping).filter(([, v]) => v && !VERILUME_BUDGET_CATEGORIES.includes(v) && !/^Client: .+/.test(v));
      if (invalid.length) return sendJson(res, 400, { error: `Unknown Verilume category: ${invalid.map(([k, v]) => `"${v}" for "${k}"`).join(', ')}` });
      const now3 = new Date().toISOString();
      Object.entries(body.mapping).forEach(([rawCategory, verilumeCategory]) => {
        if (!verilumeCategory) return;
        db.prepare('UPDATE marketing_budget_line_items SET verilumeCategory = ? WHERE uploadId = ? AND category = ?').run(verilumeCategory, uploadId, rawCategory);
        // Round 132bx follow-on, per direct instruction: "remember previous
        // upload mapping decisions by account and automatically map the
        // next time recognized." Every real (non-blank) mapping choice
        // here — whether the client accepted a suggestion or picked/typed
        // something different — is remembered per account so the same raw
        // category text auto-suggests this same choice on a future upload.
        // Recorded per-category as each choice comes in (not gated on the
        // whole upload reaching allMapped), since a partially-resolved
        // upload's individual decisions are still real and worth keeping.
        mbuRememberCategoryMapping(accountId, rawCategory, verilumeCategory);
      });
      // Defense-in-depth, per direct bug report ("I don't have a way to map
      // Total Marketing"): the import step (POST .../confirm) now refuses
      // to insert a total-like-labeled row at all, but this excludes any
      // such row on this specific upload from ever blocking confirmation —
      // in case it was imported before this fix, or via any path this
      // doesn't anticipate. A total-like row is simply never required to
      // have a mapping, the same as it's never shown as something to map.
      const distinctCategories = db.prepare('SELECT DISTINCT category FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ?').all(uploadId)
        .map(r => r.category).filter(cat => !mbuIsTotalLikeCategoryLabel(cat));
      const stillUnmapped = distinctCategories.filter(cat => {
        const row = db.prepare('SELECT verilumeCategory FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ? AND category = ? LIMIT 1').get(uploadId, cat);
        return !row || !row.verilumeCategory;
      });
      const allMapped = stillUnmapped.length === 0;
      db.prepare('UPDATE marketing_budget_uploads SET status = ?, confirmedAt = ? WHERE id = ?')
        .run(allMapped ? 'confirmed' : 'pending_category_mapping', allMapped ? now3 : upload.confirmedAt, uploadId);
      // Round 132bx follow-on — this confirm is also the "activation"
      // moment for Campaign Creation's channel picker: a category with real
      // (nonzero) confirmed dollars behind it activates its mapped
      // channel(s); one that's $0 or was never in this file leaves them
      // inactive. See recomputeAccountActiveChannels / VERILUME_TO_MMM_CHANNEL_MAP.
      let activeChannels = null;
      if (allMapped){
        const byVerilumeCategory = computeByVerilumeCategoryForUpload(uploadId);
        activeChannels = recomputeAccountActiveChannels(accountId, byVerilumeCategory);
      }
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploadId, status: allMapped ? 'confirmed' : 'pending_category_mapping', available: allMapped, stillUnmapped, activeChannels });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/active-channels — Round 132bx follow-on. Reports
    // which Campaign Creation (MMM_CATEGORIES) channels this account's
    // latest confirmed Marketing Budget Upload identified real spend
    // behind, per recomputeAccountActiveChannels. restricted:false / null
    // activeChannels means "no confirmed upload has ever run for this
    // account, don't restrict anything" — the deliberate default so every
    // account/campaign that predates this feature (or simply hasn't done a
    // Marketing Budget Upload yet) keeps its full channel picker, not an
    // empty one.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'active-channels'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      // Round 132bx follow-on (2026-08-15), per direct bug report: "The Add
      // Category does not show the new video category segments" (Linear TV
      // wasn't showing as addable in Campaign Creation even after the
      // Video/CTV -> Linear TV crosswalk fix). Root cause: this endpoint
      // used to read account_active_channels, a cache written ONLY at the
      // moment an upload is confirmed (recomputeAccountActiveChannels,
      // called from POST .../confirm-categories). Adding 'Linear TV' to
      // VERILUME_TO_MMM_CHANNEL_MAP was a correct code change, but any
      // account whose upload was already confirmed BEFORE that change
      // shipped has a stale cached row set that predates it — the new
      // mapping only would have applied on the NEXT confirm, not
      // retroactively to upload data that was already sitting there. Fixed
      // by recomputing live from the latest confirmed upload's real
      // category totals every time this is requested, against whatever the
      // CURRENT VERILUME_TO_MMM_CHANNEL_MAP is — so a crosswalk fix like
      // this one is retroactive automatically, with no re-confirm or
      // migration step needed. The account_active_channels table/write
      // path (recomputeAccountActiveChannels) is left in place as a cache
      // for now rather than removed outright, but this endpoint no longer
      // trusts it.
      // Round 132bx follow-on (2026-08-16), per direct bug report: "Apply
      // Media Mix ignores the budget categories that were uploaded." Budgets
      // are now year-scoped (see the Marketing Budget Upload year-scoping
      // round), but this endpoint still only ever looked at whichever
      // confirmed upload was most recent account-wide — the right answer for
      // Campaign Creation (which isn't itself year-scoped) but the wrong one
      // for a specific Media Plan year, where a DIFFERENT year's confirmed
      // upload could silently win. An optional ?year=YYYY narrows the lookup
      // to that year's own confirmed upload specifically; omitted, this
      // keeps the original account-wide "most recent" behavior so Campaign
      // Creation's existing call site is unaffected.
      const yearRaw = url.searchParams.get('year');
      const year = yearRaw ? parseInt(yearRaw, 10) : null;
      const latestConfirmed = year
        ? db.prepare("SELECT id FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE accountId = ? AND year = ? AND status = 'confirmed' ORDER BY confirmedAt DESC LIMIT 1").get(accountId, year)
        : db.prepare("SELECT id FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE accountId = ? AND status = 'confirmed' ORDER BY confirmedAt DESC LIMIT 1").get(accountId);
      if (!latestConfirmed){
        return sendJson(res, 200, { restricted: false, activeChannels: null });
      }
      const byVerilumeCategory = computeByVerilumeCategoryForUpload(latestConfirmed.id);
      const active = new Set(MMM_CHANNELS_ALWAYS_ACTIVE);
      Object.entries(byVerilumeCategory).forEach(([vc, total]) => {
        if (!(total > 0)) return;
        (VERILUME_TO_MMM_CHANNEL_MAP[vc] || []).forEach(ch => active.add(ch));
      });
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT fileName FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ?').get(latestConfirmed.id);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { restricted: true, activeChannels: [...active], uploadId: latestConfirmed.id, uploadFileName: upload ? upload.fileName : null });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads — history, most recent
    // first. GET .../marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId — one upload's full
    // analysis plus (if confirmed) its committed line items, rolled up by
    // category so the client sees real dollar totals, not just raw rows.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const rows = db.prepare('SELECT id, fileName, layout, status, year, createdAt, confirmedAt FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all(accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { uploads: rows });
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const analysis = JSON.parse(upload.analysisJson);
      const lineItems = db.prepare('SELECT category, status, month, amount, annualTotal, verilumeCategory FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ? ORDER BY category, month').all(uploadId);
      const byCategory = {};
      lineItems.forEach(li => {
        if (!byCategory[li.category]) byCategory[li.category] = { category: li.category, status: li.status, verilumeCategory: li.verilumeCategory || null, months: {}, annualTotal: li.annualTotal ?? null, sumOfMonths: 0 };
        if (li.month) { byCategory[li.category].months[li.month] = li.amount; byCategory[li.category].sumOfMonths += (li.amount || 0); }
        if (li.annualTotal != null) byCategory[li.category].annualTotal = li.annualTotal;
      });
      // Only meaningful once every category is mapped (upload.status ===
      // 'confirmed') — the real "available to use" view, rolled up by
      // Verilume's own taxonomy instead of each client's raw wording.
      // A client's own accepted/edited total (see category-overrides above)
      // replaces the computed sum for that category in the final "available
      // to use" rollup — the underlying line items are untouched, only what's
      // reported here changes. See computeByVerilumeCategoryForUpload.
      const byVerilumeCategory = upload.status === 'confirmed' ? computeByVerilumeCategoryForUpload(uploadId) : {};
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        id: upload.id, fileName: upload.fileName, layout: upload.layout, status: upload.status, year: upload.year,
        createdAt: upload.createdAt, confirmedAt: upload.confirmedAt,
        note: analysis.note, blocks: analysis.blocks, monthsRecognized: analysis.monthsRecognized,
        categories: Object.values(byCategory),
        byVerilumeCategory: upload.status === 'confirmed' ? byVerilumeCategory : null,
        available: upload.status === 'confirmed'
      });
    }

    // DELETE /api/accounts/:id/marketing-budget-uploads/:uploadId — round
    // 132bx follow-on (2026-08-16), per direct instruction: "User should
    // manually adjust uploaded campaigns or delete as a last resort. Delete
    // requires confirmation when campaigns are assigned to budget." ("manually
    // adjust" = the existing category-overrides screen, reused for a
    // confirmed upload too — no code change needed there, it was never
    // gated on upload.status.) This is the "last resort" path: removes the
    // upload's line items, category overrides, and the upload record
    // itself, freeing that year up for a fresh upload.
    // Body: { confirmDelete: boolean }. If this account has a Media Plan
    // for the upload's year with real campaigns drawing against it
    // (fundingSource='plan', funded from an allocation on that plan — via
    // either the legacy single allocationId or the newer
    // campaign_allocation_draws join), deleting the budget behind that
    // plan could orphan those campaigns' funding story, so a first call
    // without confirmDelete=true returns 409 with the affected campaign
    // list instead of deleting anything — the front end shows that list
    // and requires an explicit second call with confirmDelete:true to
    // actually proceed. No affected campaigns → deletes on the first call,
    // no confirmation theater needed.
    if (req.method === 'DELETE' && parts.length === 5 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'marketing-budget-uploads'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      const uploadId = parts[4];
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const upload = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ? AND accountId = ?').get(uploadId, accountId);
      if (!upload) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'no such marketing budget upload on this account' });
      const body = await readBody(req).catch(() => ({}));
      let affectedCampaigns = [];
      if (upload.year){
        const plan = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM media_plans WHERE accountId = ? AND year = ?').get(accountId, upload.year);
        if (plan){
          const allocIds = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM media_plan_allocations WHERE mediaPlanId = ?').all(plan.id).map(r => r.id);
          if (allocIds.length){
            const placeholders = allocIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
            const viaLegacy = db.prepare(`SELECT id, name FROM campaigns WHERE accountId = ? AND fundingSource = 'plan' AND allocationId IN (${placeholders})`).all(accountId, ...allocIds);
            const viaDraws = db.prepare(`SELECT DISTINCT c.id, c.name FROM campaigns c JOIN campaign_allocation_draws d ON d.campaignId = c.id WHERE c.accountId = ? AND d.allocationId IN (${placeholders})`).all(accountId, ...allocIds);
            const byId = {};
            [...viaLegacy, ...viaDraws].forEach(c => { byId[c.id] = c; });
            affectedCampaigns = Object.values(byId);
          }
        }
      }
      if (affectedCampaigns.length && !body.confirmDelete){
        return sendJson(res, 409, {
          requiresConfirmation: true,
          affectedCampaigns,
          count: affectedCampaigns.length,
          message: `${affectedCampaigns.length} campaign${affectedCampaigns.length === 1 ? ' is' : 's are'} funded from ${upload.year}'s Media Plan, which this budget feeds. Deleting it won't delete those campaigns, but their funding will no longer trace back to a confirmed budget for ${upload.year}.`
        });
      }
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM marketing_budget_category_overrides WHERE uploadId = ?').run(uploadId);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM marketing_budget_line_items WHERE uploadId = ?').run(uploadId);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE id = ?').run(uploadId);
      // The active-channels cache (recomputeAccountActiveChannels) was last
      // written from whichever upload was confirmed most recently — if that
      // was THIS one, recompute from whatever confirmed upload (any year)
      // is next most recent, or clear it if none remain, so a deleted
      // budget's channels don't linger as "active" with nothing behind them.
      const nextConfirmed = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM marketing_budget_uploads WHERE accountId = ? AND status = 'confirmed' ORDER BY confirmedAt DESC LIMIT 1").get(accountId);
      if (nextConfirmed){
        const byVerilumeCategory = computeByVerilumeCategoryForUpload(nextConfirmed.id);
        recomputeAccountActiveChannels(accountId, byVerilumeCategory);
      } else {
        db.prepare('DELETE FROM account_active_channels WHERE accountId = ?').run(accountId);
      }
      return sendJson(res, 200, { deleted: true, uploadId, year: upload.year });
    }

    // getEffectiveMmmTotals) rolled up by period + partner bucket label (see
    // mmm_category_mappings above), for handing off to whatever MMM partner/
    // tool is eventually chosen. Spend/Reach/Impressions are summed within a
    // bucket (always numeric, well-defined to add). Outcome is deliberately
    // NOT summed across raw categories, and — since campaign line items
    // carry no outcome fields of their own — only ever comes from manually-
    // logged rows: different categories can report different, incompatible
    // outcome metrics (e.g. "Conversions" vs. "Leads"), and silently adding
    // those together would misrepresent the data — so each raw category's
    // outcome is listed separately under `outcomes` instead.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-partner-export'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      const mapping = getMmmCategoryMapping(accountId, included);
      const bucketByCategory = Object.fromEntries(mapping.map(m => [m.rawCategory, m.partnerBucketLabel]));
      const { totals, manualRows } = getEffectiveMmmTotals(accountId);
      const buckets = {}; // key: periodLabel + '||' + bucketLabel
      totals.forEach(t => {
        const bucketLabel = bucketByCategory[t.category] || t.category;
        const key = `${t.periodLabel}||${bucketLabel}`;
        if (!buckets[key]){
          buckets[key] = { periodLabel: t.periodLabel, partnerBucketLabel: bucketLabel, spend: 0, reach: 0, impressions: 0, rawCategories: [], outcomes: [] };
        }
        const b = buckets[key];
        if (t.spend != null) b.spend += t.spend;
        if (t.reach != null) b.reach += t.reach;
        if (t.impressions != null) b.impressions += t.impressions;
        if (!b.rawCategories.includes(t.category)) b.rawCategories.push(t.category);
      });
      manualRows.forEach(r => {
        const bucketLabel = bucketByCategory[r.channel] || r.channel;
        const key = `${r.periodLabel}||${bucketLabel}`;
        const b = buckets[key];
        if (b && (r.outcomeMetric != null || r.outcomeValue != null)){
          b.outcomes.push({ rawCategory: r.channel, outcomeMetric: r.outcomeMetric, outcomeValue: r.outcomeValue });
        }
      });
      const exportRows = Object.values(buckets).sort((a, b) => a.periodLabel === b.periodLabel
        ? a.partnerBucketLabel.localeCompare(b.partnerBucketLabel)
        : a.periodLabel.localeCompare(b.periodLabel));

      // Round 132bk — adstock/lag estimates only ever leave the app for a
      // raw category a human has explicitly marked 'applied' (see
      // mmm_adstock_lag_decisions above and the analysis-readiness endpoint
      // below where that decision gets made). 'reference' and 'removed'
      // categories are left out of the export entirely — reference estimates
      // stay internal until someone approves them, and removed ones are a
      // known false read that should never reach a partner. Attached once
      // per bucket (a bucket can consolidate multiple raw categories, e.g.
      // OTV+CTV into "Streaming Video" — each applied raw category gets its
      // own entry so the partner sees exactly which one it's for).
      const decisions = getMmmAdstockLagDecisions(accountId);
      const appliedCategories = Object.keys(decisions).filter(cat => decisions[cat].status === 'applied' && included.includes(cat));
      let anyApplied = false;
      exportRows.forEach(row => {
        const appliedForBucket = row.rawCategories.filter(cat => appliedCategories.includes(cat));
        row.appliedAdstockLag = appliedForBucket.map(cat => {
          anyApplied = true;
          const est = computeAdstockLagEstimate(accountId, cat);
          return { rawCategory: cat, ...est, decision: decisions[cat] };
        });
      });
      const adstockLagExportNote = anyApplied
        ? `appliedAdstockLag fields are this build's own empirical, single-parameter grid-search estimates of lag and adstock decay from the account's own logged data — not a fitted MMM model — approved for inclusion here by a human reviewer (see each entry's decision.decidedBy/decidedAt/reason). Provided as directional signal only; your own modeling process should derive its own parameters.`
        : null;

      return sendJson(res, 200, { rows: exportRows, adstockLagExportNote });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-analysis-readiness — round 87: a real,
    // computed data-sufficiency readout for this account's logged MMM data,
    // plus the static recommended-test-design reference per included
    // category (see TEST_DESIGNS_BY_CATEGORY above). Every number here is
    // arithmetic over this account's own real logged data — manual mmm_inputs
    // rows AND (round 89) the campaign-sourced roll-up, combined via
    // getEffectiveMmmTotals — never a fitted model, lift estimate, or
    // invented statistic. Thresholds (24-period ideal, 12-period minimum,
    // 0.15 coefficient-of-variation floor, 0.8 correlation ceiling) are
    // commonly-cited MMM practitioner rules of thumb, stated as such — not
    // this build's own invented bar, and not a substitute for the eventual
    // modeling partner's own judgment.
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-analysis-readiness'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT mmmIncludedCategoriesJson FROM accounts WHERE accountId = ?').get(accountId);
      if (!account) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'account not found' });
      const included = account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson ? JSON.parse(account.mmmIncludedCategoriesJson) : [];
      const { totals: rows } = getEffectiveMmmTotals(accountId); // rows: one per period+category, .channel replaced by .category below

      const mean = arr => arr.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / arr.length;
      const stdev = arr => { const m = mean(arr); return Math.sqrt(mean(arr.map(v => (v - m) ** 2))); };
      const pearson = (a, b) => {
        const ma = mean(a), mb = mean(b);
        const num = a.reduce((s, v, i) => s + (v - ma) * (b[i] - mb), 0);
        const den = Math.sqrt(a.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - ma) ** 2, 0) * b.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - mb) ** 2, 0));
        return den === 0 ? null : num / den;
      };

      const periodList = [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.periodLabel))].sort();
      const periodCount = periodList.length;
      const timeDepth = {
        periodCount,
        assessment: periodCount >= 24 ? 'adequate' : periodCount >= 12 ? 'borderline' : 'insufficient',
        note: 'MMM practitioners commonly cite roughly 24+ periods (e.g. 2 years of monthly data) as a comfortable base, with 12-24 considered borderline; this is industry rule-of-thumb guidance, not a hard rule this build asserts.'
      };

      // Per-category coverage + spend variation, computed only over rows
      // this category actually has on file (never padded with zeros).
      const categoryCoverage = included.map(cat => {
        const catRows = rows.filter(r => r.category === cat && r.spend != null);
        const periodsLogged = catRows.length;
        if (periodsLogged === 0){
          return { category: cat, periodsLogged: 0, hasAnyData: false, spendMean: null, spendCv: null, flag: 'no_data' };
        }
        const spends = catRows.map(r => r.spend);
        const m = mean(spends);
        // A category logged with zero spend every period (e.g. an owned/earned
        // channel like Internal Email or PR that's deliberately run at $0
        // media spend) has no spend variation to assess at all — that's a
        // distinct, more useful flag than "ok", which would otherwise be
        // misleading (there's no spend for MMM to correlate against outcome).
        if (m === 0){
          return { category: cat, periodsLogged, hasAnyData: true, spendMean: 0, spendCv: null, flag: 'zero_spend' };
        }
        const cv = stdev(spends) / m;
        let flag = 'insufficient_data';
        if (periodsLogged >= 3){
          flag = cv < 0.15 ? 'low_variation' : 'ok';
        }
        return { category: cat, periodsLogged, hasAnyData: true, spendMean: m, spendCv: cv, flag };
      });

      // Cross-category collinearity risk: correlate spend series between
      // every included pair over their shared logged periods (needs at
      // least 3 shared periods to be meaningful).
      const collinearityRisk = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < included.length; i++){
        for (let j = i + 1; j < included.length; j++){
          const catA = included[i], catB = included[j];
          const byPeriodA = Object.fromEntries(rows.filter(r => r.category === catA && r.spend != null).map(r => [r.periodLabel, r.spend]));
          const byPeriodB = Object.fromEntries(rows.filter(r => r.category === catB && r.spend != null).map(r => [r.periodLabel, r.spend]));
          const commonPeriods = Object.keys(byPeriodA).filter(p => byPeriodB[p] !== undefined);
          if (commonPeriods.length < 3) continue;
          const seriesA = commonPeriods.map(p => byPeriodA[p]);
          const seriesB = commonPeriods.map(p => byPeriodB[p]);
          const correlation = pearson(seriesA, seriesB);
          if (correlation != null && Math.abs(correlation) >= 0.8){
            collinearityRisk.push({ categoryA: catA, categoryB: catB, commonPeriods: commonPeriods.length, correlation });
          }
        }
      }

      const testingOptions = included.map(cat => ({ category: cat, testDesigns: TEST_DESIGNS_BY_CATEGORY[cat] || [] }));

      // Round 132bj/132bk — adstock (carryover/decay of media spend into
      // later periods) and lag (delayed response) signal, per direct
      // instruction. Real, computed diagnostics over this account's own
      // logged spend + outcome values — a single-parameter grid search
      // picking whichever lag (0-3 periods) or geometric decay rate (0-0.9)
      // best correlates transformed spend against outcome — never a fitted
      // multi-variable MMM model. Each estimate now also carries a real
      // Fisher z-transformation significance test (see computeAdstockLagEstimate
      // above) as the "confidence score" a human data scientist can use to
      // decide whether to lean on it, plus that human's own decision
      // (reference/applied/removed — see mmm_adstock_lag_decisions above),
      // defaulting to 'reference' until someone explicitly decides otherwise.
      const decisions = getMmmAdstockLagDecisions(accountId);
      const adstockLagEstimates = included.map(cat => {
        const estimate = computeAdstockLagEstimate(accountId, cat);
        estimate.decision = decisions[cat] || { status: 'reference', reason: null, decidedBy: null, decidedAt: null };
        return estimate;
      });
      const adstockLagNote = `Empirical, single-parameter grid-search estimates from this account's own logged spend and outcome values per category (minimum 6 periods with both logged) — assumes logged periods are consecutive, equal-length time periods. Confidence is a real Fisher z-transformation significance test on the correlation, most reliable with more periods. This is directional signal, not a fitted MMM model: a real model with proper confidence intervals across every variable at once requires the eventual MMM partner's own modeling process. A human data scientist should review each estimate and mark it Reference (stays internal), Apply (included in the partner export), or Remove (a false read — excluded everywhere, with a logged reason).`;

      return sendJson(res, 200, { included, timeDepth, categoryCoverage, collinearityRisk, testingOptions, adstockLagEstimates, adstockLagNote });
    }

    // GET /api/accounts/:id/mmm-adstock-lag-decisions — current decision
    // per category plus the full append-only history (round 132bk).
    if (req.method === 'GET' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-adstock-lag-decisions'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
      if (!requireAccount(req, res, accountId)) return;
      const decisions = db.prepare('SELECT category, status, reason, decidedBy, decidedAt FROM mmm_adstock_lag_decisions WHERE accountId = ?').all(accountId);
      const history = db.prepare('SELECT category, status, reason, decidedBy, decidedAt FROM mmm_adstock_lag_decision_log WHERE accountId = ? ORDER BY decidedAt DESC').all(accountId);
      return sendJson(res, 200, { decisions, history });
    }

    // POST /api/accounts/:id/mmm-adstock-lag-decisions — a human data
    // scientist recording Reference / Apply / Remove for one category's
    // adstock/lag estimate. 'removed' requires a reason (the "why was this a
    // false read" record) — everything else, reason is optional. Every call
    // both upserts current state and appends to the log, so a category that
    // gets removed then later reinstated still shows the full history rather
    // than silently forgetting the earlier removal.
    if (req.method === 'POST' && parts.length === 4 && parts[0] === 'api' && parts[1] === 'accounts' && parts[3] === 'mmm-adstock-lag-decisions'){
      const accountId = decodeURIComponent(parts[2]);
